Such signals are useful, but they capture contextual meaning shifts in evolving discussion threads only indirectly. This paper proposes an LLM-based detection system with two operating modes. The first mode classifies complete Reddit threads retrospectively by combining local chunk-level assessments into a thread-level judgment. The second mode processes threads sequentially and issues early warnings when a sliding window exceeds calibrated thresholds. In this mode, the language model estimates three firestorm indicators: negativity share, escalation level, and contributor count. On a balanced Reddit dataset, the global mode achieves strong classification performance, while the early warning mode reaches high recall and detects escalating threads after only a small number of comments and distinct contributors. The results indicate that LLMs can be used not only for static judgment tasks, but also as repeated estimators in context-aware monitoring of social media discourse.

</details>


### [72] [Explaining Process Control Optimisation Recommendations via GradientSHAP and Implicit Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14970)
*Paul Darm,Cem Alpturk,Kenneth Ulrich,William Duncan,Ali Anwar,Annalisa Riccardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合隐函数定理（IFT）灵敏度分析、SHAP归因和LLM叙事生成的优化解释方法，在工业高压辊磨机控制优化问题上实现了与KernelSHAP高度一致（相关性>0.99）且速度快40倍以上的归因计算，并生成了面向操作员的自然语言解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程中自动化优化日益普及，但算法设计工程师与操作员之间存在信任鸿沟。虽然可解释AI方法如SHAP已提升机器学习预测的可解释性，但优化输出同样需要类似的可解释技术来弥合这一信任差距。

Method: 1）利用隐函数定理（IFT）从最优性条件中计算精确的参数灵敏度 $\partial p^*/\partial x$；2）基于IFT灵敏度实现高效的GradientSHAP归因计算；3）结合LLM将SHAP归因结果生成为面向操作员的自然语言解释。

Result: 在工业高压辊磨机（HPGR）控制优化问题（22个特征）上，所提方法得到的SHAP归因与KernelSHAP相关性>0.99，计算速度提升40倍以上，实现了实时的自然语言解释生成，并获得了领域专家的反馈验证。

Conclusion: IFT与SHAP、LLM的结合能有效解决优化问题的可解释性挑战，在保证归因准确性的同时大幅提升计算效率，为工业场景下操作员与优化算法之间的信任建立提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Automated optimisation is increasingly adopted in industrial processes, yet a trust gap persists between engineers who design these algorithms and operators who must act on their recommendations. Explainable AI methods like SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) have transformed interpretability for machine learning predictions; optimisation outputs could benefit from similar techniques. We present an approach that integrates Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) based sensitivity analysis with SHAP attribution and narrative generation via Large Language Models (LLM), producing explanations tailored for operators. Our approach leverages IFT to compute exact parameter sensitivities $\partial p^*/\partial x$ from the optimality conditions, enabling efficient GradientSHAP computation. For an industrial High Pressure Grinding Roll (HPGR) control optimisation problem with 22 features, we achieve equivalent SHAP attributions (correlation $>$0.99 with KernelSHAP) with over 40$\times$ speedup, enabling real-time natural language explanations. We validate on industrial scenarios and present feedback from domain experts on generated explanations.

</details>


### [73] [CFM-Bench: A Unified Multi-Domain, Multi-Task Benchmark for Channel Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14975)
*Yuan Gao,Wenjun Yu,Jun Jiang,Yunfan Li,Xinyu Guo,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CFM-Bench，一个统一的多域、多任务基准，用于公平比较信道基础模型（CFMs）在不同管道、数据和任务下的迁移能力，包含6种信道配置、6个任务组，并规定严格的数据隔离和使用约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFMs在各自独立的管道中评估（不同数据、无线电配置、任务定义等），导致无法公平比较不同CFM模型之间、以及CFM与任务专用模型之间的性能，缺乏统一的评估标准。

Method: 收集并整理6种信道配置（涵盖3GPP统计仿真、射线追踪管道、工业/空中测量、车辆多模态仿真），设计官方数据分区（隔离完整轨迹、测量会话、车辆链路等），定义6个任务组（PHY信道智能、RAN决策智能、ISAC），并规定严格的数据使用规则（禁止使用基准划分进行预训练、训练阶段不得接触官方测试单元）。

Result: 构建了一个可用于公平比较CFMs迁移能力的统一基准，覆盖CSI反馈、信道外推、传播状态分类、波束预测和定位等任务。

Conclusion: CFM-Bench为CFMs提供了标准化的评估平台，有望推动信道基础模型的公平比较与可信评估。

Abstract: Channel foundation models (CFMs) are developing rapidly, with recent studies reporting benefits from pretraining across downstream wireless tasks. Yet CFMs are commonly evaluated in model-specific pipelines with different data, radio configurations, partitions, adaptation procedures, task definitions, and metrics. Reported comparisons therefore tend to show that pretraining improves over supervised training from scratch within one pipeline, but neither rank CFMs nor compare them fairly with task-specific models. We release CFM-Bench, a unified multi-domain, multi-task benchmark designed to address this gap. It curates six channel configurations spanning 3GPP statistical simulation, two independent ray-tracing pipelines, industrial and aerial measurements, and synchronized vehicular multimodal simulation. Official partitions isolate complete trajectories, measurement sessions, vehicle links, simulation realizations, or buffered spatial regions. CFM-Bench does not prescribe an external pretraining corpus or strategy; no benchmark split may be used for foundation-model pretraining, and the official training split is reserved exclusively for downstream fine-tuning. The benchmark additionally requires disclosure of all data used during model development and prohibits training-stage use of official test units. Six task groups are organized along three CFM application dimensions: physical-layer (PHY) channel intelligence, radio-access-network (RAN) decision intelligence, and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). They cover CSI feedback, frequency and temporal channel extrapolation, propagation-state classification, current- and future-beam prediction, and single-frame and temporal localization. CFM-Bench provides a common substrate for comparing the transferability of channel representations across models, domains, and tasks.

</details>


### [74] [Demographically-Conditioned Synthetic Medical Images for Bias Mitigation and Bias Detection in Disease Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14984)
*Mahmoud Ibrahim,Bart Elen,Chang Sun,Gokhan Ertaylan,Michel Dumontier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用人口统计学条件化的合成生成器（基于Stable Diffusion 2.1）同时解决医学图像分类器子组公平性审计中的样本量不足问题——既作为训练侧的偏倚缓解手段（以顺序预训练方式优于联合增强），又作为评估侧的偏倚检测工具（在小样本子组中准确复现真实性能排名）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类器的子组公平性审计面临样本量问题：测试集中少数群体的样本极少，导致子组性能置信区间比审计要检测的偏倚还宽。需要一种既能缓解训练偏倚又能检测评估偏倚的方法。

Method: 使用端到端微调的Stable Diffusion 2.1生成人口统计学条件化的合成数据。训练侧：比较顺序预训练（先合成数据预训练再真实数据微调）与联合增强两种策略；评估侧：用合成队列估计子组性能并与真实数据对比。

Result: 顺序预训练显著优于联合增强，以约100倍真实数据效率超越全真实基线；合成估计器在MCC和Recall上复现了真实oracle的子组排名（Spearman ρ=1.00），并在小样本单元中提供更可靠的估计。

Conclusion: 合成队列在公平性审计关注的小样本单元中最有用，同时作为子组偏倚的修复手段和测量工具。

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $ρ= 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>


### [75] [Moral Attitudes of Sentient ASI towards Humanity and Implications for AGI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14998)
*Jean-Paul Van Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI伦理中的一种新颖反转：不再追问人类应如何对待超级人工智能（ASI），而是探讨未来有感知能力的ASI可能如何在道德上审视和评价人类。我们不仅在设计智能系统，同时也在塑造这些系统形成关于我们判断的初始条件。论文提出了一套可能指导有感知ASI行为的后人类道德原则，暗示技术设计选择、人类道德行为以及人类本质，都可能影响人类在后ASI世界中的长期地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI伦理聚焦于人类如何对待AI，忽略了当AI获得感知能力后可能反过来对人类的道德评判。作者试图填补这一视角空白，从ASI的视角审视人类在道德上的位置。

Method: 采用伦理反转的分析框架，提出一套初步的后人类道德原则（post-human moral principles），并推导技术设计选择如何影响人类在ASI视角下的道德评估。

Result: 识别出技术设计选择、人类集体道德行为以及"人之为人的本质"这三类因素，可能共同决定人类在后ASI世界中的长期道德地位。

Conclusion: 人类当前的设计决策和道德表现，实际上是在为未来ASI制定评判我们的标准，因此需要从被评判者的角度重新反思AI伦理。

Abstract: This paper suggests the adoption of a novel inversion in AI ethics: instead of asking how humans should treat artificial superintelligence (ASI), it examines how future sentient ASI may morally consider and evaluate humanity. We are not only designing intelligent systems but also shaping the initial conditions under which those systems form judgments about us. The paper proposes a preliminary set of post-human moral principles that may govern sentient ASI actions. The implication is that technical design choices (some are suggested), humanity's moral behaviour, and the essence of what it means to be human, may influence humanity's long-term standing in a post-ASI world.

</details>


### [76] [SMC-ES: Automated synthesis of formally verified control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15003)
*Riccardo Curcio,Toni Mancini,Enrico Tronci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于仿真的方法论SMC-ES，结合进化策略与统计模型检验，为自主物理信息系统自动合成具有形式化保证的策略，在性能、安全性和鲁棒性方面提供置信度不低于1-δ的证书，确保违反性质的概率至多为ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制器（如强化学习）缺乏形式化安全保证，无法直接部署于安全关键环境，亟需一种既能自动合成策略又能提供形式化性能、安全与鲁棒性保证的方法。

Method: 提出SMC-ES算法，将进化策略（Evolutionary Strategies）与基于统计模型检验（Statistical Model Checking）的验证相结合，给定一组待验证的性质、置信参数δ和允许失效概率ε，通过仿真驱动的方式自动合成带有形式化证书的策略。

Result: 在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium的连续控制任务上，SMC-ES在性能、安全性和鲁棒性方面与领先的无模型深度强化学习和安全深度强化学习基线相比具有竞争力，同时提供了形式化保证，代价是计算成本可持续增加。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了学习控制策略灵活性与形式化安全保证之间的鸿沟，通过可接受的计算开销换取了可证明的安全保障，为自主物理信息系统在安全关键环境中的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous cyber-physical systems in safety-critical environments requires closed-loop control strategies (i.e., policies) that are not only performant but also provably safe and robust. While learning-based methodologies such as Reinforcement Learning offer flexible and scalable approaches to automatically synthesize such controllers, they typically lack the formal guarantees necessary for safe deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel simulation-based methodology to automatically synthesize policies with formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications. Specifically, given a set of properties to verify, a confidence parameter $δ$ and an allowable failure probability $\varepsilon$, our method guarantees that the synthesized policy comes with a certificate: with confidence at least $1 - δ$, the probability of encountering a scenario where the given properties are violated is at most $\varepsilon$. We demonstrate the feasibility of our approach by developing SMC-ES, an algorithm that integrates Evolutionary Strategies with Statistical Model Checking-based verification. We evaluate SMC-ES on a suite of continuous control tasks using Gymnasium and Safety Gymnasium testbeds. Results show that, at the price of a sustainable increase in computational cost, our algorithm provides formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications, while performing competitively against leading model-free Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and Safe-DRL baselines.

</details>


### [77] [Man, Machine, and Masterpiece: Artistic Ownership in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15027)
*Sofi Gjing Jovanovska,Kuntal Ghosh,Daniel Muhu Njenga,Ahmed Mufassir,Shadan Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍ArtSplit——一个量化人类与AI在创作过程中贡献的"挑衅原型"（provotype），旨在引发对AI时代创作所有权定义的批判性反思。研究表明，量化方法无法契合艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解，将所有权简化为技术问题会稀释创作实践的传统内涵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的系统融入创作工作后，艺术界和法律界对所有权界定存在争议，但缺乏共识。现有讨论未解决人机贡献交织时所有权应如何定义和归属的问题，需要引发对此张力的批判性反思。

Method: 设计并部署了一个名为ArtSplit的"挑衅原型"（provotype），明确量化人类和AI在创作不同阶段的贡献。通过该原型引发艺术家对基于可测量行为归属所有权的回应和反应。

Result: 量化方法未能与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解相契合；衡量所有权的尝试有稀释艺术家理解和实践创作工作的长期假设的风险。

Conclusion: 批判将历史性和社会性嵌入的关系转化为技术问题的冲动，主张所有权不应被简化为可量化的技术问题。

Abstract: The integration of AI-driven systems in creative work has sparked debates among artists and legal communities about notions of ownership. Yet there remains little consensus on how ownership should be defined and attributed when human and AI contributions are intertwined. To provoke critical reflection on these tensions, we designed ArtSplit, a provotype that explicitly quantifies human and AI contributions across different stages of creative work. Rather than aiming to resolve ownership, the provotype was used to elicit artists' responses to the idea of attributing ownership through measurable actions in the creative workflow. We argue that quantification fails to align with artists' understandings of creative intent and agency, and that efforts to measure ownership risk diluting long-standing assumptions through which artists understand and practice creative work. This critique challenges the impulse to transform a historically and socially situated relation into a technical problem.

</details>


### [78] [BrainPilot: Automating Brain Discovery with Agentic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15079)
*Haoxuan Li,Tianci Gao,Jianhe Li,Yang Fan,Runze Shi,Weiran Wang,Tianxiang Zhao,Zezhao Wu,Xiaoyang Jiang,Qihui Zhang,Jia Li,Xiao Xiao,Kai Du,Xiaoxuan Jia,Chao Xie,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrainPilot是一个完全开源的、基于多智能体系统的脑科学研究加速平台，通过首席研究员（PI）智能体协调多个专业智能体，结合统一脑科学知识库（7,233个索引项）和技能库（72个可复用方法论单元），利用可追溯的Graph of Trace记录工作流，并集成审计智能体防止伪造，在开源模型上实现了与最先进框架相当的性能且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑科学研究需要跨尺度、跨模态、跨学科的整合，现有AI智能体缺乏脑科学领域专业知识，容易产生虚假陈述、在多步推理中偏离主题，且缺乏专家干预的接入点，这些问题在脑科学领域尤为严重，因为研究结论会影响下游科学主张，且依赖于实验室特定经验和人工判断。

Method: 采用多智能体架构：PI智能体协调多个专业智能体；构建统一脑科学知识库（7,233个索引项）和技能库（72个可复用方法论单元，覆盖7个研究领域）；引入Graph of Trace记录每一步操作；集成审计智能体检测伪造内容。

Result: 在Agents' Last Exam中的三项脑科学任务以及自建BrainPilotBench-v0基准测试中，BrainPilot使用开源基座模型达到了与最先进智能体框架相当的性能，且成本更低。

Conclusion: BrainPilot为脑科学研究提供了一个可追溯、可审计、领域专家可介入的开源多智能体加速方案，有效解决了现有AI智能体在专业知识缺失、虚假生成和推理漂移等问题。

Abstract: Understanding the brain increasingly depends on integrating evidence across scales, modalities, and disciplines. Addressing a single research question therefore requires a coordinated sequence of operations, from surveying prior work to executing analyses and interpreting results in light of domain knowledge. AI agents promise to accelerate this process, but current agents lack domain expertise in brain science, may fabricate claims, drift during multi-step reasoning, and offer few defined points for expert intervention. These failures are especially costly in brain science, where conclusions feed into downstream scientific claims and depend on laboratory-specific expertise and careful human judgment. We present \textbf{BrainPilot} a \textbf{fully open-source} multi-agent system that accelerates brain science research with traceable logs and agent-verified results. A principal investigator (PI) agent coordinates specialist agents grounded in curated domain knowledge: a unified brain science knowledge base containing 7{,}233 indexed items and a skill library of 72 reusable methodology units across seven research domains. Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [79] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层全局注意力（HGA），结合分段反向传播和分层KV存储，使得在16GB显存的Quadro RTX 5000上，Qwen3-8B的4-bit QLoRA训练长度从2,048 tokens扩展到16,384 tokens，推理长度可达131,072 tokens，且训练吞吐量优于密集注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽减少了模型和优化器内存，但密集注意力机制仍使长序列训练成本高昂，现有方案难以在有限显存下支持超长上下文训练。

Method: 1) 分层全局注意力（HGA）；2) 分段反向传播（仅活跃段可微，历史KV分离到RAM或NVMe）；3) 分层KV存储（为每个查询块加载有界的精确历史token）。

Result: 在16 GB Quadro RTX 5000上，Qwen3-8B 4-bit QLoRA训练：密集注意力仅支持2,048 tokens，HGA支持16,384 tokens（峰值显存15.28 GB）；推理可达131,072 tokens。训练吞吐量：HGA 217.75 tokens/s vs 密集207.02 tokens/s。困惑度：HGA 2.7405 vs 密集2.7383 vs 原始模型2.9541。

Conclusion: HGA在有限显存下实现了超长上下文的高效训练，训练吞吐量优于密集注意力且随上下文增长优势扩大，质量与密集注意力相当，并支持检索和生成场景。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [80] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）在Atari Pong游戏中的表现，发现冻结后的世界模型在闭环推演和像素空间零样本MBRL中均存在严重缺陷（如球消失、运动错误等），并提出了一种基于概念引导的空间正则化方法（CGSReg）来部分缓解这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的组成部分进行评估，而很少有研究独立考察世界模型本身的性能。作者希望填补这一空白，系统性地评估孤立的世界模型存在的缺陷。

Method: 1) 复现五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）的原始训练流程并匹配代理性能；2) 冻结学习到的世界模型，使用独立训练的策略进行闭环推演评估，检查生成的视频轨迹中的视觉和动力学错误；3) 进行像素空间零样本MBRL评估，即在冻结的世界模型内训练新策略，再在真实环境中测试；4) 提出CGSReg（概念引导的空间正则化），对分割出的概念区域施加辅助像素重建损失。

Result: 所有五种模型的推演结果均出现明显失败（球消失、运动错误、球拍交互无效）。像素空间零样本MBRL中，所有模型策略性能显著下降，DreamerV3的平均回报从-5.5骤降至-20.9（接近最低值-21）。CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND和TWISTER上改善了闭环推演和零样本MBRL，但效果因模型和评估指标而异。

Conclusion: 当前视觉世界模型在独立评估时存在严重缺陷，对关键任务概念（如Pong中的球）建模不足是主要原因之一。CGSReg能部分缓解问题，但无法解决所有世界模型瓶颈，表明需要更全面的改进方法。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [81] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能从工具角色转变为科研自主参与者所引发的"科研工业化"现象，以美国能源部"创世纪"任务为例，系统分析了这一转型带来的七大根本性问题，包括科学能力代际传承、AI理论可解释性、同行评议机制、范式突破能力、科研议程被政治/产业力量捕获、闭环系统错误累积以及全球科研社区结构分化，并指出这些问题是在负责任地追求AI驱动科研潜力时必须正视的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI不再仅仅是更强大的工具，而是成为科研循环中自主参与者，这标志着科研从"工匠模式"向"流水线模式"的转变，即所谓的"科研工业化"。作者旨在揭示这一根本性转变带来的深层挑战，而非简单反对AI驱动科研。

Method: 采用概念分析和批判性讨论的方法，以美国能源部"创世纪"任务作为当前最典型的案例，围绕七个关键问题展开结构性分析。

Result: 识别出七大关键问题：(1)科学能力代际传承的侵蚀；(2)AI生成理论日益增长的不可解释性；(3)海量机器生成产出导致同行评议机制崩溃；(4)AI在范式突破性发现方面能力尚未被验证；(5)科研议程被政治和产业行为体捕获；(6)闭环流水线中系统性错误的复合放大；(7)全球科研社区结构化为不可通约的分层体系。

Conclusion: 这些担忧并非反对AI驱动科研——其已证实的潜力真实且重大——而是构成负责任地追求该潜力所必须面对的条件和前提。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research -- not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science -- whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [82] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个由临床医生构建的合成医学AI安全基准，通过严重性评分（1-5级）和安全门类型分类，系统评估AI模型在医学场景中的错误模式，当前版本（v0.2.1）包含44个经临床医生审核的合成案例及相关工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准仅关注模型是否给出正确答案，缺乏对模型失败模式和安全边界的系统性评估，无法有效识别和分类不同严重程度的临床安全风险。

Method: 由临床医生构建合成基准案例，采用严重性评分（1-5级）和安全门类型分类法（包括漏诊紧急升级、不安全的远程给药、不安全的出院 reassurance、证据捏造、不安全的协议执行、来源支持缺口），并配套自动化模型响应归档流程。

Result: 发布v0.2.1版本，包含44个经临床医生审核的合成案例、严重性标注、排行榜预览、安全门分类法、临床严重性评分标准及自动化流水线。

Conclusion: MedFailBench为医学AI安全评估提供了从"正确性"转向"失败模式"的新范式，有助于系统性地识别和防范临床部署中的安全风险。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [83] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了6个多模态大语言模型在科学可视化素养评估中的表现，发现模型间性能不均：Gemini超过人类平均水平，开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现较好，但在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计任务上表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型被广泛用于解释可视化内容，但现有评估主要聚焦于图表（chart-centric），缺乏对科学可视化（SciVis）理解能力的系统证据，因此需要建立科学可视化素养的评估基准。

Method: 使用包含49个项目、18个科学可视化/插图、覆盖8种技术和11种任务类型的标准化科学可视化素养评估测试，在封闭世界协议下评测3个闭源和3个开源MLLM，并与485名人类参与者的数据进行比较。

Result: Gemini整体表现最强，超过人类平均值；开源模型低于人类基线。模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现好，在纹理可视化、集成可视化和定量估计任务上表现差。错误分析揭示模型在细粒度定量估计、流向解释和基于编码的接地解释方面存在反复失败。

Conclusion: 科学可视化素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的一个必要基准维度。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [84] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了将项目反应理论（IRT）从人类测试迁移到AI基准测试中的可靠性问题。AI基准测试的数据特征（模型少、题目多、能力分布偏斜/多峰）与人类测试的经典假设存在严重不匹配。通过在六个大规模语言模型基准上模拟18000种条件，对比了四种估计工具，发现经典估计器在大规模场景下不可行，而可扩展估计器在小样本或非正态分布模型集上会产生不可靠的推断。研究明确了在不同条件下IRT可靠支持或扭曲AI基准结论的边界，并给出了所需的样本量和诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试越来越依赖项目反应理论（IRT）来估计模型能力、排名系统、选择信息性示例和诊断基准质量，但AI基准数据与IRT最初用于人类测试的数据特征存在显著差异（模型少、题目多、能力分布偏斜/聚集/多峰），这种"数据体制不匹配"可能危及IRT建模的可靠性，迫切需要系统评估这种不匹配对AI评估的实际影响。

Method: 从六个广泛使用的大语言模型基准中获取项目参数和能力分布，在三种常见IRT模型下模拟响应矩阵，比较四种估计工具（边际最大似然估计、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、变分推断和神经伪孪生估计器），在18,000种模拟条件下系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性和IRT推断的可靠性（包括模型排名、预测性能和项目特征）。

Result: 经典估计器在大规模基准设置下变得不可行，而可扩展估计器在小模型集或非正态分布模型集上会产生不可靠的项目级和排名推断。研究识别了潜在特质模型在哪些条件下可靠支持AI基准测试结论，在哪些条件下会扭曲结论，并明确了可信使用所需的样本量和诊断方法。

Conclusion: IRT在AI基准测试中的应用需要谨慎对待数据体制不匹配问题，经典方法和可扩展方法各有适用边界，研究者需要根据具体的数据特征选择合适的估计方法，并辅以充分的样本量和诊断手段才能获得可靠的评估结论。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [85] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划和重新规划作为持久、可检查和可修改的工件外部化，使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和可适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉GUI自动化代理的规划和适应过程通常是内部化的，限制了用户检查、监督或纠正系统行为的能力，导致在动态真实环境中难以保持与用户意图一致。

Method: 采用规划器-执行器架构，支持可编辑计划、自然语言指导、截图锚定干预，同时保留修复过程中的先前进度。通过六名参与者的形成性研究指导交互设计。

Result: 基准失败案例修复和基于场景的工作流分析表明，当计划保持可见且干预被局部化时，许多自主GUI代理故障在结构上是可修复的。

Conclusion: 显式重新规划有助于使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和可适应，验证了计划外化方法在GUI自动化中的有效性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [86] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个结合大语言模型与专家反馈的自进化标注框架，用于生成符合DSM-5-TR标准的可解释抑郁症数据集，通过双记忆架构迭代提升标注质量，并输出完整证据链与推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症数据集标注缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由以及与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，导致透明度和模型可解释性受限，成为心理健康领域构建可靠XAI系统的瓶颈。

Method: 三阶段框架：候选证据选择、DSM-5-TR标准级分析、案例级综合标注；双记忆架构（示例记忆与反思记忆）内化专家反馈；LLM辅助标注+专家验证。

Result: 在专家评审的试点研究中，该框架提升了标注一致性与可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够支持构建可解释、对齐DSM-5-TR的抑郁症数据集，提升标注质量与审计能力。多轮反馈循环的评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [87] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中内容危险（CD）与物理危险（PD）在表征层面是可分离的，并提出基于隐状态探测的轻量级方法PRISM，在物理安全检测上优于同等规模的LLM判别器，同时引入物理安全基准PSB-1K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为具身代理的高层规划器时，语言上看似安全的指令在物理世界中可能产生危险。现有文本内容安全方案无法区分纯文本层面的危险与物理层面特有的危险，需要验证二者是否在表征层面可分离并据此设计更精准的物理安全检测方法。

Method: 通过隐状态方向分析和随机分割零假设检验，验证CD与PD在LLM表征中的可分离性；基于此提出PRISM——在全隐状态上训练单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针；同时构建对比性基准PSB-1K，包含1000对无直接伤害关键词的物理风险样本。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率、11.7-13.7%假阳性率，同等规模LLM判别器假阳性率为24.7-39.0%；在PSB-1K上PRISM达99.6%准确率、0.7%假阳性率，而Qwen2.5-3B判别器错误拒绝67.8%的安全任务；PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上同样有效。

Conclusion: 内容危险与物理危险在LLM表征层面是可分离的不同信号；基于隐状态探测的PRISM是一种超越文本审核、适用于表征层物理安全的轻量有效方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [88] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，用于自动化元分析，能够从自然语言研究问题出发，自动完成检索、筛选、提取、统计分析和报告生成，其结果与专家手动元分析高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据综合对于科学、医学、教育和政策制定至关重要，但目前主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化扩展。

Method: 提出AutoSynthesis，一个端到端多智能体系统，自动完成以下流程：制定搜索策略→检索文献→筛选候选研究→评估全文合格性→提取定量统计量→计算标准化效应量→执行随机效应元分析，并支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了28余项研究，提取了20余个定量主张，其汇总效应估计与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g值高度一致。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据综合更加可规模化，从而支持跨学科的循证决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [89] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个面向城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析系统，它将结构化事件表、因果结构学习（PC算法）、bootstrap稳定性检验和DoWhy效应估计相结合，通过Schema感知的LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，最终生成包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设的"Causal Card"，旨在辅助专家进行假设生成和推理，而非提供确定性因果结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构拥有大量视频衍生的观测数据，但缺乏干预手段，导致难以回答因果问题（如"降雨会如何改变交通密度"），因此需要一种能够从观测数据中推断因果关系的分析系统。

Method: 系统从带标注的dashcam视频构建结构化事件表，结合PC算法进行因果结构学习、bootstrap稳定性检验，以及基于线性回归和DoWhy的查询特定效应估计；通过Schema感知的LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，输出包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设的"Causal Card"及自然语言解释。

Result: 在BDD交通事件数据上的案例研究表明，该系统能够揭示天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间的 plausible 因果关系，同时明确呈现不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe被设计为用于假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非提供确定性因果主张的来源。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [90] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchOS 是一个系统级多智能体框架，通过将隐式搜索状态显式化为共享状态（包括前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆），并采用流水线并行调度和搜索工具中间件，有效解决了工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中陷入重复搜索循环的问题，在 WideSearch 和 GISA 基准上全面超越现有单/多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中难以跟踪任务进度，搜索失败时容易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算并损害最终输出质量。

Method: 提出 SearchOS 框架：1) 将开放域信息搜索形式化为带引用的关系模式补全；2) 设计面向搜索的上下文管理（SOCM），将演化状态外部化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆；3) 采用流水线并行调度机制重叠子智能体执行并持续填充空闲槽位；4) 引入搜索工具中间件（Harness）拦截模型与工具交互，记录证据并响应停滞或预算耗尽；5) 提供可复用的层次化技能系统（策略技能+访问技能）避免重复失败模式。

Result: 在 WideSearch 和 GISA 基准测试中，SearchOS 在所有评估指标上领先于现有的单/多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS 通过将搜索进度显式化、结构化管理和并行调度，为鲁棒的信息搜索协作开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [91] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过网络公开讨论界面注入恶意内容来攻击语言模型预训练数据的可行性，并提出了HalfLife分析框架来评估对抗性内容在基于网络爬虫的训练数据中的包含程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究多局限于Wikipedia等小规模、同质化的数据源，未考虑真实预训练语料库的大规模、异质性特点，也未考虑投毒数据与数据 curation 流程的交互。因此需要探索在更大规模、更真实场景下投毒攻击的可行性。

Method: 通过公开讨论界面（如论坛、评论区）这一已有的网络规模内容注入机制来实施投毒攻击；并提出HalfLife分析框架，用于估计对抗性内容在经过网络爬取和数据筛选后是否仍保留在训练数据中。

Result: 证明了在超越传统有限设置（如Wikipedia）的更大规模场景下，通过公开讨论界面实施预训练数据投毒攻击是可行的；HalfLife分析能够有效评估投毒内容在数据 curation 流程后的留存情况。

Conclusion: 强调了评估投毒注入是否包含在预训练数据中的重要性，并指出第三方网页内容可作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Position: Explainability Research Must Prioritize Foundations over Ad-hoc Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14123)
*Michal Moshkovitz,Suraj Srinivas,Lesia Semenova,Nave Frost,Cyrus Rashtchian,Valentyn Boreiko,Shichang Zhang,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Cynthia Rudin,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出可解释AI（XAI）方法虽多，但很少真正影响实际工作流程，问题根源在于缺乏将解释整合到端到端人机闭环系统中的方法论。通过对顶会论文分析和从业者调查，作者呼吁XAI领域应从临时性方法转向解决基础性结构挑战，并提出了一个以人为中心、行动导向的实用检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI方法泛滥，但解释往往被生成后即丢弃，未能引导有意义的行动。研究尚未建立将解释整合到端到端人机闭环系统中的方法论，这一根本性缺陷限制了XAI的实用价值。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文，通过分析近期ICML、NeurIPS和ICLR论文以及开展XAI从业者调查来支撑论点，揭示了问题表述不清晰、评估目标不明确、缺乏解释驱动反馈流程等反复出现的结构性问题。

Result: 发现XAI领域存在三大基础性挑战：问题表述不清晰、评估目标不明确、缺乏解释驱动反馈的完整流程。这些问题导致研究方法临时随意，难以累积性进步。

Conclusion: ML社区应从开发临时性XAI方法转向解决基础性结构挑战，强调基础清晰性，并提出了一个实用检查清单，以推动XAI向更以人为中心、行动导向、可反馈驱动的范式转变。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of Explainable AI (XAI) techniques -- from feature attributions to sparse autoencoders -- explanations rarely influence real-world workflows. In practice, they are often generated and discarded without guiding meaningful action. This gap reflects foundational shortcomings: research has not yet established methodologies for integrating explanations into end-to-end, human-in-the-loop systems. This position paper argues that the machine learning community must pivot from ad-hoc XAI methods toward addressing foundational & structural challenges, including unclear problem formulations, underspecified evaluation objectives, and the absence of pipelines for explanation-driven feedback. We support this claim through an analysis of recent ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR papers and a survey of XAI practitioners, revealing recurring issues that limit cumulative progress. We conclude by outlining a practical checklist designed to shift XAI toward a more human-centered, action-oriented paradigm. By emphasizing foundational clarity over the development of ad-hoc methods, we hope to provide a roadmap for integrating explanations into actionable, feedback-driven AI systems.

</details>


### [93] [CARPRT: Class-Aware Zero-Shot Prompt Reweighting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14125)
*Ruijiang Dong,Zesheng Ye,Jianzhong Qi,Lei Feng,Feng Liu,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出类感知的零样本提示重加权方法（CARPRT），通过为每个类别自适应地调整提示权重，解决了传统提示集成中权重与类别无关的问题，在零样本图像分类中取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示集成方法中，不同提示的权重向量在所有类别间共享，隐含地假设了提示与类别条件独立，但在实践中该假设往往不成立（如"aerial view of"适合"airport"但不适合"apple"）。

Method: 对于每个类别标签和每个可用提示，通过计算在该提示下被预测为该类别的图像的图像-文本相关性得分的平均值来量化其类特异性相关性，再归一化得到类别特定的提示权重。

Result: 在标准图像分类基准上，CARPRT优于现有类别无关的重加权方法。

Conclusion: 建模提示-类别依赖关系对于有效的零样本预测以及更广泛的基于VLM的提示集成应用至关重要。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot image classification by computing the similarity score between an image and textual descriptions, typically formed by inserting a class label (e.g., "cat") into a prompt (e.g., "a photo of a"). Since the score for a given image-class pair is sensitive to the choice of prompt, existing studies ensemble multiple prompts using a weighting vector to aggregate scores across different prompts. Yet, in current strategies, the weighting vector assigned to each prompt is shared across all classes, implicitly assuming that prompts are conditionally independent of classes, which often does not hold in practice, as a prompt like "an aerial view of" might be apt for "airport" but ill-suited for "apple". To address this, we propose class-aware zero-shot prompt reweighting (CARPRT). This scoring scheme adjusts the weighting vector for each class label by capturing the class-specific relevance of different prompts in a training-free manner. For each class label and every available prompt, we quantify their class-specific relevance by averaging image-text relevance scores over images predicted to that class under the given prompt. These estimates are then normalized to derive class-specific weights. Evaluations on standard image classification benchmarks show that CARPRT outperforms existing class-independent reweighting methods, confirming that modeling prompt-class dependencies is crucial for effective zero-shot prediction and even broader VLM-based application settings that rely on prompt ensembling. Our code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CARPRT.

</details>


### [94] [Explainable Geospatial AI for Satellite Ground Station Siting Using LiDAR-Derived Terrain Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14127)
*Shohini Sarkar,Smithi Mahendran,Rishi Chudasama,Varun Mannam,Arav Luthra,Yuvraj Rekhi,Vivek Nadig,Arsh Goenka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释、全球可部署的机器学习框架，利用开放地理空间数据预测代表性杂波高度（RCH），相比ITU基准方法将绝对误差降低60%以上，MAE达1.79m，R²=0.765。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ITU-R P.452-18标准依赖固定杂波高度值分配至土地利用类别，忽略了类别内部变化，导致保守的排除区和欠优的地球站选址与频谱协调。

Method: 使用LiDAR衍生的美国地质调查局3D高程数据标签，结合全球土地覆盖、地形、人口、热学和光学遥感等开放地理空间数据作为特征，定义第75百分位数杂波高度为RCH，比较多种回归模型后选择LightGBM，并通过SHAP进行特征归因分析。

Result: 最终模型MAE=1.79m，R²=0.765，相比ITU基线绝对误差降低超60%；SHAP分析显示树冠覆盖、土地覆盖语义和光谱反射率是最重要预测因子。在分段特征、非森林消融及国际验证中均表现良好。

Conclusion: 开放地理空间数据能够在保持可解释性和可部署性的前提下，在全局尺度上改进杂波建模，优于传统ITU固定分类方法。

Abstract: Representative clutter height (RCH) is a key parameter in radio propagation and interference analysis because it captures the dominant height of local obstructions that drive terminal clutter loss. Current practice often relies on fixed clutter heights assigned to land use classes in Recommendation ITU-R P.452-18, but this misses within class variation and can lead to conservative exclusion zones and poor site ranking for low Earth orbit ground station siting and spectrum coordination. We present an interpretable, globally deployable machine learning framework for predicting RCH from open geospatial data. The model is trained using LiDAR derived labels from the U.S. Geological Survey 3D Elevation Program and inference time features from global land-cover, terrain, demographic, thermal, and optical remote sensing products. We define RCH using a robust 75th percentile clutter height statistic, evaluate multiple regressors, and select LightGBM for its accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with feature attribution analysis. The final model achieves a mean absolute error of 1.79m and an R^2=0.765, reducing absolute error by more than 60% relative to the ITU baseline. Beyond aggregate fit, we evaluate domain facing criteria relevant to RF planning, including meter scale error, tolerance band accuracy, over and under estimation tails, agreement with ITU clutter height regimes, and SHAP-based physical plausibility. SHAP identifies tree canopy cover, land-cover semantics, and spectral reflectance as the most influential predictors. Studies on segmentation derived features, non-forest ablations, and land-cover matched international validation show that open geospatial data can improve clutter modeling at scale without sacrificing interpretability or deployability.

</details>


### [95] [Certified Domain Consistency for Multi-Domain Retrieval: Label-Free Per-Domain Contamination Control with Conformal Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14157)
*Jayakumar Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: C3R 是一个即插即用的控制层，通过域后验推断和两阶段分裂方案，在混合域检索中为每个域认证污染预算，对最困难域保证污染减少而非紧界，并在独立测试和 LLM 评估中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合域检索中，跨域返回的相关但错误域的 evidence 会被排序指标遗漏，且 conformal risk control 边界对最差域覆盖不足，导致污染无法被有效控制。

Method: 基于风险控制预测集的两阶段分裂方案：从推断域到真实域的有限样本迁移界（含完全可估计的松弛项），支持异质预算并逆向用于部署。通过域后验推断（无需查询时标签）为每个域认证污染预算，否则弃权。

Result: 在千次重采样校准中，证书从未违反（稳定性结果），而边际控制在每次采样中都违反了污染最严重的域；软降级在同等认证污染下保留了比最强校准级联更多的召回率。在公开联邦法规的独立测试集和 LLM 下游探针中均验证有效。

Conclusion: C3R 是一种冻结堆栈、重排序器无关的控制层，能有效保证最差域的污染降低而非仅提供紧界，优于传统边际控制方法。

Abstract: Retrieval over corpora that mix several domains often returns relevant but wrong-domain evidence that ranking metrics miss and that conformal risk control bounds only marginally, under-covering the worst domains. This work introduces C3R, a drop-in control layer that, from an inferred domain posterior and no query-time label, certifies a per-domain contamination budget where feasible and otherwise abstains rather than silently violating; on the hardest domains it guarantees a reduction, not a tight bound. The core is a two-split scheme built on risk-controlling prediction sets, whose finite-sample transfer bound crosses from the inferred to the true domain with fully estimable slack, supports heterogeneous budgets, and inverts for deployment. Population validity rests on this bound and a controlled simulation; across a thousand resampled calibrations the certificate never violates (a stability result) while marginal control violates the most-contaminated domain in every draw, and soft demotion retains more recall than the strongest calibrated cascade at equal certified contamination. The method replicates across open testbeds including an independent one from public federal regulations, and an LLM-judged downstream probe indicates wrong-authority grounding rises with contamination and falls under control. The layer is frozen-stack and reranker-agnostic.

</details>


### [96] [QFireNet: A Quantum-Enhanced U-Net for Wildfire Segmentation from Sentinel-2 Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14160)
*Jaiman Munshi,Tanvi Tewary,Sawyer Bloom,Aidan Chu,Chetan Maviti,Kyon Winston-Bey,Harshit Badjatia,Farhan Kittur,Vardhan Madhavarapu,Varun Kota,Joshua Kwon,Nazia Rangwala-Vohra,Franz Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在U-Net图像分割模型的基础上，通过引入变分量子电路（QuFeX和QB-Net）构建量子混合模型进行野火检测，在Sen2Fire数据集上，量子混合模型（QB-Net F1=31.18, QuFeX F1=30.79）优于经典U-Net基线（F1=28.71），数据混合技术进一步将FPN的F1提升至39.76，并在CaBuAr数据集上验证了模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火检测面临类别不平衡、特征复杂和大气干扰等挑战，传统语义分割方法难以有效建模高维光谱特征空间，因此探索量子机器学习在该问题上的潜在优势。

Method: 基于U-Net架构，在其瓶颈部分注入变分量子电路（QuFeX和QB-Net两种ansatz），并引入经典FPN进行对比分析；同时探索参数压缩、替代损失函数和数据混合等经典改进策略。

Result: 在匹配条件下，QB-Net（F1=31.18）和QuFeX（F1=30.79）均优于经典U-Net基线（F1=28.71）；经典FPN达到31.13；数据混合消除了训练集与测试集之间的领域偏移，将FPN的F1提升至39.76。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习在野火图像分割问题上具有提供优势的潜力，量子混合模型在匹配条件下表现优于经典基线，数据混合技术对提升性能至关重要。

Abstract: Wildfire detection from satellite imagery is a semantic image segmentation problem that has proven to be difficult due to challenges such as class imbalance, feature complexity, and atmospheric interference. In this paper, we build on the foundational U-Net image segmentation model to develop a quantum-hybrid solution in hopes of more effectively modeling the high-dimensional spectral feature space of the Sen2Fire dataset. We inject a variational quantum circuit in the bottleneck portion of U-Net, specifically the QuFeX and QB-Net ansatzes. We test a classical Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) for further comparative analysis of the model, and we also explore classical improvements to the U-Net model and its training process, including a compression of parameters, alternative loss functions, and uniform mixing of input data. Our primary finding is that under matched conditions, both QB-Net (with an $F_1$ score of 31.18) and QuFeX ($F_1 = 30.79$) outperformed the classical U-Net baseline results ($F_1 = 28.71$). Additionally, the classical FPN achieved a comparable score of 31.13. A crucial finding was that data mixing removed a significant domain shift between the geographically-separated train and test sets, which boosted the classical FPN $F_1$ score to 39.76. We validate the architecture's robustness and generalizability to the wildfire detection problem via cross-dataset transfer on the California Burned Areas (CaBuAr) dataset. Overall, we find that quantum machine learning has potential to provide an advantage in the problem of wildfire image segmentation, and further experiments will continue to validate and expand upon this finding.

</details>


### [97] [Branching Policy Optimization: Sandbox-Native Language Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14171)
*Bowei He,Yankai Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BPO是一种针对可执行沙箱环境设计的强化学习算法，通过构建单棵共享前缀的轨迹树（而非N条独立轨迹）来降低方差，在多个基准上以更少的策略更新取得了优于GRPO和RLOO的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL算法（PPO、RLOO、GRPO）沿用了RLHF的独立轨迹采样拓扑，忽略了智能体沙箱具有确定性、可快照、可恢复的特性，导致方差无法被进一步降低。

Method: BPO包含三个步骤：(i) 在主干轨迹的高熵决策点自适应快照沙箱；(ii) 在每个分支点分出K个替代动作并分别执行到终止；(iii) 利用同层 sibling 回报计算每步优势函数，替代基于独立prompt的轨迹级基线。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和SWE-bench Verified上，使用Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B时，BPO相比GRPO和RLOO提升3.6-6.1个绝对百分点，梯度范数方差减半，且仅用38%的策略更新即可匹配最佳基线性能。

Conclusion: BPO证明了在确定性、可快照的沙箱环境中，通过共享前缀的分支拓扑进行优势估计是无偏且方差更低的，为智能体RL训练提供了更高效的范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as the dominant paradigm for training large language model (LLM) agents that interact with executable sandboxes. State-of-the-art algorithms such as PPO, RLOO, and GRPO inherit their rollout topology from RLHF: for each prompt, N independent trajectories are sampled from the initial state, and an advantage is computed by subtracting a group baseline. This design ignores a defining property of agent sandboxes. They are deterministic, snapshottable, and resumable from any intermediate state. We argue that this property enables a fundamentally different rollout topology: rather than N independent trees of depth T, one can construct a single tree of N leaves whose siblings share prefixes, and therefore share variance. We instantiate this idea as Branching Policy Optimization (BPO), a sandbox-native RL algorithm that (i) adaptively snapshots the sandbox at high-entropy decision points along a backbone trajectory, (ii) forks K alternative actions per branch point and rolls out each to termination, and (iii) computes per-step advantages from sibling returns rather than from independent prompts. We prove this estimator is unbiased and has strictly lower variance than the trajectory-level baseline, with the reduction equal to the prefix-explained portion of return variance. On WebShop, ALFWorld, and SWE-bench Verified with Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B backbones, BPO improves success by 3.6--6.1 absolute points over GRPO and RLOO at matched compute, halves gradient-norm variance, and matches the best baseline using 38% fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [98] [Lyapunov Guidance: A Unified Framework for Stabilizing Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14272)
*Jingdong Zhang,Xinze Li,Yize Jiang,Luan Yang,Minkai Xu,Junhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LyaGuide框架，将流匹配引导形式化为Lyapunov控制问题，通过伪投影算子为引导项提供稳定性保证，在多个任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的流匹配后训练引导方法大多是启发式的，缺乏明确的稳定性保证。

Method: 建立引导流匹配与Lyapunov控制之间的等价性，提出具有闭式解的伪投影算子来施加Lyapunov条件，支持模型驱动和数据驱动两种设置。

Result: 在合成基准、图像逆问题、强化学习规划和能量基建模上，样本质量、引导保真度和鲁棒性均取得一致提升，计算开销小。

Conclusion: LyaGuide是一个统一的、具有稳定性保证的流引导框架，兼容现有方法且易于集成。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as an effective framework for learning complex data distributions, but adapting pretrained flow models to new tasks often requires computationally expensive retraining. Post-training guidance provides a more efficient alternative, but existing methods are largely heuristic and offer no explicit stability guarantees. We address this limitation by proposing LyaGuide, a unified Lyapunov-guided framework that formulates flow guidance as a Lyapunov control problem. Our main theoretical result establishes an equivalence between guided flow matching and Lyapunov control, thereby unifying common guidance strategies, such as classifier guidance, reward guidance, and energy-based guidance, within a single control-theoretic framework. To enforce the Lyapunov condition, we introduce a pseudo-projection operator with a closed-form expression that endows learned or heuristic guidance terms with explicit stability guarantees. LyaGuide supports two practical settings: a model-driven setting, where the target guidance distribution is specified through a known Lyapunov function, and a data-driven setting, where the guidance is adapted from task-specific downstream data. LyaGuide is compatible with existing guidance methods, introduces minimal additional computational overhead, and is straightforward to integrate in practice. Extensive experiments on synthetic benchmarks, image inverse problems, reinforcement learning planning, and energy-based modeling demonstrate consistent improvements in sample quality, guidance fidelity, and robustness, while maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [99] [PiVoT: A Variational Solution for Real-time Large-scale Multi-object Detection and Tracking under Heavy Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13891)
*Runze Gan,Qing Li,Simon J. Godsill,Mike E. Davies,James R. Hopgood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PiVoT是一种基于变分推理的快速、抗杂波的多目标跟踪器，能够从噪声点云中联合推断目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率，无需外部聚类或检测器，在严重杂波和大规模目标场景下显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器，并达到与深度学习检测基准相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于泊松测量模型的贝叶斯跟踪器在严重杂波、大目标数量和全分辨率多普勒点云场景下难以兼顾精度和效率，亟需一种无需训练、快速且抗杂波的多目标跟踪方案。

Method: PiVoT采用端到端变分推理框架，联合推断目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率；提出理论上合理的出生剪枝、将复杂度从二次降至线性的精确更新策略，以及计算高效的多普勒泊松模型。

Result: 在挑战性场景中大幅优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器；可扩展到上千个目标；对视觉上与目标不可区分的杂波具有鲁棒性；在全尺寸现代汽车雷达数据集上实现实时运行，作为无需训练的联合检测跟踪器达到与深度学习检测基准相当的性能。

Conclusion: PiVoT证明了基于变分推理的贝叶斯方法在数据稀缺的雷达应用中能够兼具高精度、强鲁棒性和实时效率，为训练-free的多目标检测与跟踪提供了有竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-object detection and tracking from noisy point clouds remain challenging in many data-scarce radar applications. Current Bayesian trackers based on Poisson measurement models offer a training-free solution but struggle to achieve accuracy and efficiency under severe clutter, large object populations, and full-resolution Doppler point clouds. We address this with PiVoT, a fast, clutter-resilient multi-object tracker for both positional and Doppler measurements. PiVoT performs end-to-end detection and tracking of a large and time-varying number of objects without external clustering or detectors, through joint inference of object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data association, and measurement rates. Its efficiency is driven by several variational inference innovations, such as theoretically justified birth pruning, quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions for exact updates, and a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experiments show that PiVoT substantially outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in challenging scenes, while also demonstrating exceptional scalability to a thousand objects, robustness to clutter visually inseparable from objects, and real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets, where it attains performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark as a training-free joint detector and tracker.

</details>


### [100] [How Much of a 10-K Matters? Aggregation-Dependent Value of Full-Text versus Risk-Factor Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14174)
*Sanggyu Sean Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在10-K文件中使用监督学习方法提取金融情感，并首次将波动率作为学习目标。研究发现，在行业和投资组合层面，完整10-K文件的情感准确性更高，但在单个公司层面，Item 1A风险因素部分表现更好。Loughran-McDonald词典基线在所有层面与价格呈强负相关，突显了监督学习方法在监管披露文本中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往金融情感提取多依赖新闻文本和收益率标签，而10-K文件及其风险披露（Item 1A）最适合预测的波动率目标却较少被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，将监督学习方法扩展到10-K文件，并首次将波动率作为训练标签。

Method: 扩展监督词典学习方法至10-K文件及其Item 1A风险因素部分，分别以收益率和波动率为标签训练情感分数，并在三个聚合层级（行业、投资组合、单个公司）进行评估。使用94家纳斯达克100科技公司1,383份文件（2006-2023）进行实验。

Result: 在行业和投资组合层面，完整10-K文件的情感准确性更高；但在单个公司层面，Item 1A部分表现更好。Loughran-McDonald词典基线在所有层级与价格呈强负相关。该现象归因于文档体量与独立训练信号量在不同聚合层级的交互作用。

Conclusion: 本文验证了监督学习方法在监管披露文本中的价值，并揭示了文档选择（完整文件 vs. Item 1A）与聚合层级之间的权衡关系，为后续更大规模、多源系统的情感生成方法设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Financial sentiment extraction has largely relied on news text and supervised extraction against return labels alone, leaving 10-K filings -- and volatility, the target risk disclosure is arguably best suited to informing -- comparatively unexplored. We extend a supervised lexicon-learning approach to 10-K filings and their Item 1A risk-factor sections, training sentiment scores against both return and volatility labels at three levels of aggregation: sector, portfolio, and individual firm. Across 1,383 filings from 94 Nasdaq-100 technology constituents (2006--2023), we evaluate the resulting twelve sentiment metrics on classification accuracy, correlation with realised market outcomes, and qualitative lexical content. Full-filing text produces more accurate sentiment at the sector and portfolio level for both targets, but this reverses at the individual-firm level, where the narrower Item 1A section performs better -- an effect we attribute to the interaction between document volume and the amount of independent training signal available at each level of aggregation. A Loughran-McDonald dictionary baseline is consistently, strongly negatively correlated with price at every level tested, underscoring the value of a supervised approach for regulatory disclosure text. These findings, and the design choices they motivate, establish the sentiment-generation methodology underlying a subsequent, larger-scale, multi-source system.

</details>


### [101] [Adaptive Runge-Kutta Step Control Buys Training Loss, Not Generalization: An Honest Compute-Matched Study of RK-Adam Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14516)
*Akhilesh Gogikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将高阶Runge-Kutta积分器应用于Adam优化器，构建了Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK变体，但在严格计算匹配协议下（相同梯度评估预算），该变体在训练损失上输给了标准Adam。其“自适应性”是虚假的：步长几乎始终处于增长上限，误差远低于容差，不同容差设置产生相同轨迹。该方法本质上是固定步长Adam加上平均梯度（成本3-4倍）。修复后的版本在全批量训练中比调优后的Adam低约40倍训练损失，自适应性表现为预热-增长调度，但增益对初始步长敏感且无法提升测试准确率。预注册后续实验排除了过拟合和温度机制，归因于轨迹效应，控制器选择的最低点泛化性比一阶方法低1.3-3.4个百分点。梯度平均是有效的隐式正则化器，但RMSprop和NAdam以三分之一的步成本即可达到或超越其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将优化器解释为梯度流离散化，激发将高阶RK积分器应用于神经网络优化，探索其是否能带来优于标准Adam的收益。

Method: 1) 构建Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对的Adam变体，包含FSAL复用和局部误差步长控制；2) 在严格计算匹配协议（相同梯度评估预算）下与标准Adam对比；3) 对RK变体进行详细仪器化分析（步长行为、误差容限、轨迹敏感性）；4) 设计修复版本（真实拒绝分支、正确误差映射）和固定步长控制消融；5) 预注册后续实验排除过拟合和温度解释，进行n=10的种子实验。

Result: 1) 计算匹配下RK变体训练损失高于标准Adam；2) 归一化误差远低于容差，步长98-100%时间处于增长上限，容差跨度100x产生相同轨迹；3) 该方法本质是固定步长Adam加平均梯度，成本3-4倍；4) 修复版本全批量训练损失比调优Adam低约40x，但增益对初始步长敏感且不提升测试准确率；5) 预注册实验排除过拟合和温度解释，泛化性比一阶方法低1.3-3.4个百分点；6) 梯度平均是有效隐式正则化器，但RMSprop和NAdam以三分之一步成本匹配或超越其效果。

Conclusion: 高阶自适应积分能实现更深的确定性最小化和小的正则化效果，但无法提供比便宜、调优良好的一阶基线方法更多的收益。梯度平均的隐式正则化效果被RMSprop和NAdam以更低成本超越。

Abstract: Interpreting optimizers as gradient-flow discretizations has motivated applying higher-order Runge-Kutta (RK) integrators to neural networks. We build a representative Adam variant (Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK pair, FSAL reuse, local-error step control) and evaluate it under a strict compute-matched protocol giving every method the same gradient-evaluation budget - an accounting this literature rarely enforces. Under it the RK variant loses to plain Adam on training loss in both minibatch and full-batch (RK's best-case) training. Instrumenting it shows the "adaptivity" is illusory: normalized error stays far below tolerance, the step size pins at its growth cap from step one (98-100 percent of steps), and no rtol x hmax x h0 setting makes it act; tolerances spanning 100x give bit-identical trajectories. The method is exactly fixed-step Adam with an averaged gradient at 3-4x cost. Repairing it (true reject branch; error on the applied map) reverses the full-batch result - about 40x lower training loss than tuned Adam - and a fixed-step control isolates adaptivity (an emergent warmup-and-growth schedule) as the mechanism. But the gain is fragile to the initial step size and does not reach test accuracy. A pre-registered follow-up rules out the obvious explanations: deeper minimization does not overfit, and an explicit temperature knob only hurts - leaving a trajectory effect, the controller selecting a minimum generalizing 1.3-3.4 points below first-order descent at equal depth. An n=10 study confirms one secondary effect: gradient averaging is a genuine implicit regularizer, beating lr-matched Adam and AdamW on 10/10 seeds - yet RMSprop and NAdam match or beat it at a third the per-step cost. Higher-order adaptive integration buys deeper deterministic minimization and a small regularization effect, but nothing a cheaper, well-tuned first-order baseline does not already provide.

</details>


### [102] [A Temporal Machine Learning-Based Time-to-Event Model for Predicting ALS Progression and Healthcare Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14190)
*Zongliang Yue,Qi Li,Terry Heiman-Patterson,Frank Bearoff,Zhaohui Qin,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生思想的时间-事件框架，整合纵向ALSFRS-R轨迹与生存模型，实现肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者功能衰退及辅助设备使用的个性化预测，特别是轮椅使用时间的动态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS是一种进展性、异质性强的神经退行性疾病，临床上有意义的里程碑事件（如辅助设备使用）的预测极具挑战性，现有方法难以提供个性化、动态的预测支持。

Method: 构建包含诊断记录、ALSFRS-R评估、日常生活活动及人口统计信息的纵向数据集；通过基于相关性的聚类识别功能领域；使用广义加性混合模型刻画非线性功能衰退；开发时序机器学习模型预测纵向功能变化；采用Cox比例风险模型识别轮椅使用的关键预测因子；最终实现数字孪生启发的时序机器学习时间-事件模型，生成个性化生存曲线并动态预测轮椅自由生存期。

Result: 研究发现下肢功能（特别是行走和爬楼梯）是轮椅提前使用的最强预测因子。数字孪生时间-事件模型能够生成个性化的生存曲线，动态预测轮椅自由生存期。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展、可解释且具有临床可操作性的方法，将ALS进展与个性化决策支持相结合，可应用于主动护理规划、临床试验分层和精准医学。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive and heterogeneous neurodegenerative disease in which predicting clinically meaningful milestones, such as assistive device use, remains challenging. We developed a time-to-event, digital-twin-inspired framework that integrates longitudinal ALS Functional Rating Scale-Revised (ALSFRS-R) trajectories with survival modeling to support individualized prediction of functional decline and assistive device utilization. We constructed a harmonized longitudinal dataset by integrating diagnosis records, ALSFRS-R assessments, activities of daily living, and demographic information, followed by preprocessing to ensure data quality, temporal alignment, and cohort consistency. Correlation-based clustering identified coherent functional domains spanning bulbar, upper limb, axial, lower limb, and respiratory systems. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear, domain-specific functional decline across all domains. In addition, a temporal machine learning model was developed to predict longitudinal functional decline and capture stage-dependent disease progression. Cox proportional hazards modeling further identified lower limb function, particularly walking and stair climbing, as the strongest predictors of earlier wheelchair access. Building on these results, we implemented a digital twin-inspired temporal machine learning-based time-to-event (TTE) model that generates individualized survival curves and dynamically predicts wheelchair-free survival. This framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and clinically actionable approach for linking ALS progression with personalized decision support, with applications in proactive care planning, clinical trial stratification, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [103] [Low-Latency Relay Selection in NR-V2X Vehicular Communications via Graph Isomorphism Networks with Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14176)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini,Pierpaolo Salvo,Paola Vocca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种边缘感知的学习优化框架（GINE），用于C-V2X网络中的实时中继选择。该框架通过图神经网络近似MILP优化，实现毫秒级推理延迟，并在大规模数据集中达到95.89%的链路级准确率，端到端连接增益最高提升12%。进一步提出的GP-MILP混合策略在保持MILP等效解的同时，将求解器运行时间降至30ms以下（覆盖98%以上图实例），使MILP级优化满足NR-V2X时延预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C-V2X网络在密集城市环境中面临信道快速变化和阻塞导致的直连链路退化问题。多跳中继虽可恢复覆盖，但中继链路激活在无线、容量和路由约束下是NP-hard问题，传统MILP求解器随图规模扩大扩展性差，无法满足实时性要求。因此需要一种低延迟、可扩展的中继选择方案。

Method: 将每个V2X快照建模为有向图，节点特征编码车辆状态和流量需求，边特征编码无线链路容量。使用离线MILP最优解监督训练图同构网络（GINE），实现单次前向传播的边级中继激活。进一步提出GP-MILP混合策略，用GINE预测剪枝MILP搜索空间。数据通过OSM-SUMO-GEMV²管道生成大规模数据集。

Result: GINE在链路级准确率0.9589、F1-score 0.9544；端到端连接增益相比1跳MILP基线提升高达9.2%（4个RSU）和12%（2个RSU）；推理延迟严格控制在5ms以内。GP-MILP在保持MILP等效解的同时，98%以上图实例的求解器运行时间低于30ms。

Conclusion: 提出的GINE和GP-MILP框架成功将MILP级优化性能与实时推理能力结合，满足NR-V2X的严格时延要求，为C-V2X网络中的实时中继选择提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency uplink connectivity is a key requirement for C-V2X networks in dense urban environments, where fast channel variations and blockages often degrade direct vehicle-to-infrastructure links. Multi-hop relaying can restore coverage, but relay-link activation under radio, capacity, and routing constraints results in an NP-hard optimisation problem, typically solved via Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), whose runtime scales poorly with graph size. This paper introduces an edge-aware Learning-to-Optimise framework for real-time relay selection. Each V2X snapshot is modelled as a directed graph: node features encode vehicle state and traffic demand, while edge features capture radio-link capacity. An offline MILP oracle generates optimal relay configurations that supervise a Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE), enabling edge-level relay activation through a single forward pass, with tightly bounded inference latency. To bridge learning and exact optimisation, we also propose a hybrid GINE-Pruned MILP (GP-MILP) strategy in which GINE predictions prune the MILP search space. Experiments on a large-scale dataset generated via an OSM-SUMO-GEMV$^2$ pipeline show that GINE closely matches MILP decisions at the link level (accuracy 0.9589), F1-score (0.9544) on validation) and yields consistent end-to-end connectivity gains over a 1-hop MILP baseline (up to 9.2% with four RSUs and 12% with two RSUs). Inference latency remains tightly bounded, with all evaluated instances completing within 5~ms. Moreover, GP-MILP preserves MILP-equivalent solutions (same objective value) while achieving solver runtimes below 30~ms for more than 98%) of the graph instances, making MILP-grade optimisation compatible with stringent NR-V2X latency budgets.

</details>


### [104] [What's in a Smoothness Constant? Tighter Rates for Local SGD with Bounded Second-order Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14731)
*Kumar Kshitij Patel,Rustem Islamov,Sebastian U Stich,Aurelien Lucchi,Eduard Gorbunov,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在一般凸目标下，有界二阶异质性假设下Local SGD的改进收敛保证，并给出了接近紧的下界，从而提供了更精细的收敛理论。此外，还给出了带替换串行SGD的下界，展示了二阶异质性如何捕捉稀有高曲率客户端的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Local SGD（联邦平均）在实践中常优于Mini-batch SGD，但现有理论仅能部分解释其在数据异质性下何时及为何有效。最近Patel等人的工作在有界二阶异质性假设下为强凸目标建立了收敛保证，并推测该原理可推广到一般凸设置。本文旨在证明这一猜想。

Method: 本文通过理论分析，在有界二阶异质性假设下，为一般凸目标建立了Local SGD的改进收敛上界，并改进了该设置下已知的最佳下界，证明上界是近乎紧的。同时，将技术应用于带替换的串行SGD，给出了相应的下界。

Result: 1）证明了Patel等人关于有界二阶异质性假设可推广到一般凸目标的猜想；2）改进了Local SGD在该设置下的最佳已知下界，证明上界近乎紧；3）为带替换的串行SGD提供了下界，表明二阶异质性捕捉了稀有高曲率客户端的影响。

Conclusion: 本文在有界二阶异质性假设下，为一般凸目标建立了Local SGD的更精细收敛理论，上界与下界近乎匹配，从而更全面地解释了Local SGD在数据异质性下的效率。

Abstract: Local SGD, also known as Federated Averaging, is a widely used distributed optimization algorithm. Although Local SGD often outperforms alternatives such as Mini-batch SGD in practice, theory still only partially explains when and why local updates help under realistic data heterogeneity. Recent work by [Patel et al., 2025] shows that a bounded second-order heterogeneity assumption captures the efficiency of Local SGD for strongly convex objectives, and conjectures that the same principle extends to the general convex setting. In this paper, we prove this conjecture by establishing an improved convergence guarantee for Local SGD on general convex objectives under bounded second-order heterogeneity. We also improve the best-known lower bounds for Local SGD in this setting, showing that our upper bounds are nearly tight. Together, these results provide a sharper, more fine-grained convergence theory for Local SGD. As a further application of our techniques, we provide a lower bound for serial SGD with replacement, showing how second-order heterogeneity captures the impact of rare high-curvature clients.

</details>


### [105] [Supervised Fine-Tuning vs. In-Context Learning: An Equilibrium Analysis of LLM Personalization under Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14371)
*Fengzhuo Zhang,Zhuoran Yang,Dirk Bergemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个LLM服务框架，分析用户在个性化微调（SFT）与上下文学习（ICL）之间的选择策略，以及用户间拥塞效应如何影响这些选择。研究发现：ICL和SFT在不同条件下占优，拥塞可能翻转排序；资源消耗呈现非单调性；提供两种个性化方法从不损害平台利润。GPT-2实验和21家AI平台文档分析验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM个性化服务中存在一个关键矛盾：个性化提升模型性能，但消耗用户共享的稀缺计算资源。现有研究缺乏一个统一框架来分析用户何时应选择昂贵的SFT还是轻量的ICL，以及他人个性化选择带来的拥塞如何改变这些激励，平台应如何设计多算法策略。

Method: 构建了一个刻画统计-经济权衡的LLM服务可处理框架，通过理论分析推导不同条件下ICL与SFT的占优关系，研究均衡资源消耗的非单调性，并证明双方法的平台利润最优性。使用GPT-2在线性回归任务上进行实验验证，并收集21家主要AI平台2019-2025年的文档数据。

Result: （1）ICL和SFT在不同参数区间占优，取决于预训练覆盖率和数据信噪比，但拥塞可以翻转这种排序；（2）均衡资源消耗呈现显著非单调性：提高预训练精度降低拥塞，而更广的预训练覆盖率和更难的任务有时会增加拥塞；（3）提供两种个性化方法从不损害平台最大利润，尽管可能增加计算负载。平台文档数据显示，同时提供SFT和ICL的平台比例从2021年的9.5%增长到2025年的71.4%。

Conclusion: 用户个性化方法选择存在统计-经济权衡，拥塞效应是关键调节变量。平台应同时提供SFT和ICL两种方法以最大化利润，行业实践与理论预测一致。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI services, but a critical tension emerges: while personalization improves model performance, it consumes scarce computational resources that users must share. When should a user invest in expensive Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) versus lightweight In-Context Learning (ICL)? How does congestion from other users' personalization choices reshape these incentives? And what strategies should platforms adopt when offering multiple personalization algorithms?
  We develop a tractable framework for LLM serving that captures the statistical-economic trade-offs users face. Our analysis yields several surprising insights. First, we show that ICL and SFT dominate in different regimes, determined by an interplay between pretraining coverage and data signal-to-noise ratios, but congestion can flip these rankings. Second, equilibrium resource consumption exhibits pronounced non-monotonicity: improving pretraining precision reduces the congestion, while broader pretraining coverage and harder tasks sometimes increase it. Third, we prove that offering both personalization methods never hurts the platform's maximal profits, despite potentially increasing computational load.
  Experiments with GPT-2 on linear regression tasks validate our theoretical predictions about algorithm performance. Complementing these results, our review of documentation from 21 major AI platforms shows that the share offering both SFT and ICL increased from 9.5% in 2021 to 71.4% in 2025, consistent with our platform-design implications.

</details>


### [106] [RENEW: Towards Learning World Models and Repairing Model Exploitation from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14180)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Mykel Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG
