  In this work, we identify a critical inefficiency in the standard A/B-testing protocol: when a treatment and control policy agree on an action, the resulting outcome contributes noise but no signal regarding the treatment effect -- unnecessarily inflating confidence intervals. We propose a novel experimental protocol that exploits this policy overlap to accelerate experimentation. The key insight is to frame the randomised treatment assignment mechanism as a meta-policy, and leverage $Δ$-Off-Policy Estimation methods to obtain unbiased estimates for average treatment effects. We prove analytically that our approach recovers standard A/B-testing practices in the general case, but that its variance scales with the divergence between policies rather than raw outcome variance. Hence, we dominate the standard Difference-in-Means estimator whenever policies have common support, and the improvement is strict whenever the overlap region contributes non-zero residual variance. Empirical results corroborate these theoretical insights -- holding promise for significant impact on the real-world evaluation of recommender systems, information retrieval pipelines, and large language model interfaces.

</details>


### [140] [Auditing Fairness-Privacy Trade-offs: Subpopulation-Level Effects of Fairness-Enhancing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14607)
*Umid Suleymanov,Ilhama Novruzova,Khalid Mammadov,Natavan Hasanova,Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了公平性增强算法如何影响子群体级别的成员推断隐私风险，发现公平性干预并非均匀增加隐私风险，其影响取决于模型架构、子群体规模和缓解策略，并提出了首个统一实证框架用于子群体层面的公平-隐私-效用联合审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要研究隐私保护技术如何影响公平性，但公平性增强算法如何影响隐私泄露的反向问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将似然比攻击（LiRA）适配用于子群体审计，结合差分隐私（DP）与多种公平性增强方法，在不同模型架构和子群体规模下进行系统性实证分析。

Result: 公平性干预并非均匀增加隐私风险；DP的隐私收益和效用成本在不同子群体间分布不均；公平性、隐私和效用必须在子群体层面联合评估。

Conclusion: 提出了首个统一的实证框架，支持在实践中对子群体层面的公平性、隐私和效用进行联合审计。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) models deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare, law enforcement, and finance must satisfy not only utility requirements but also fairness and privacy guarantees. While prior work has largely examined how privacy-preserving techniques affect fairness, the inverse question-how fairness-enhancing algorithms influence privacy leakage-remains underexplored. We present the first comprehensive study of how fairness interventions affect membership inference privacy risks at the subpopulation level. By adapting the Likelihood Ratio Attack (LiRA) for subgroup auditing, we uncover privacy disparities that aggregate evaluations obscure. We further analyze how Differential Privacy (DP) interacts with fairness-enhancing methods across different categories, showing that DP's privacy benefits and utility costs are unevenly distributed across subpopulations. Our results demonstrate that fairness interventions do not uniformly increase privacy risk; their impact depends on model architecture, subgroup size, and mitigation strategy. These findings reveal that fairness, privacy, and utility must be jointly evaluated at the subpopulation level, and we introduce the first unified empirical framework to support such auditing in practice.

</details>


### [141] [Angular Gaussian Supervised Contrastive Learning for Long-Tailed Electrocardiogram Arrhythmia Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14613)
*Jin Dai,Qiuzhen Zhang,Chenyun Dai,Danmei Lan,Can Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Angular Gaussian Supervised Contrastive Learning (AG-SCL)框架，用于长尾多标签心电图诊断。该框架通过角高斯对比分支、自适应Logit调整和尾部感知增强三个模块，有效提升了罕见心律失常的检测灵敏度，在PTB-XL和Noc-ECG数据集上取得了最佳宏平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG诊断方法在处理长尾标签分布时，主要关注类别频率，忽略了不同ECG类别间方向依赖的形态变异性，导致对临床重要但罕见的异常检测可靠性不足。

Method: AG-SCL包含三个组件：(1) Angular Gaussian对比分支，在单位归一化嵌入上建模全协方差类不确定性；(2) Adaptive Logit Adjustment，学习有界标签状态特定的先验修正而非固定频率基的边距；(3) 尾部感知增强，生成保持形态的视图并保护7-25 Hz QRS主导频段。

Result: 在PTB-XL上达到：平衡准确率0.838、灵敏度0.709、特异性0.968、平均精确率0.495、5%假阳性率下的真阳性率0.778。在Noc-ECG上对应值为：0.918、0.889、0.947、0.488、0.900。罕见或形态不稳定的心律类别提升最大。

Conclusion: AG-SCL通过将先验校准与各向异性表示学习相结合，在保持临床相关特异性的同时增强了对罕见心律失常的敏感性，有效改进了长尾ECG诊断。

Abstract: Long-tailed label distributions reduce the reliability of deep learning for electrocardiogram (ECG) arrhythmia diagnosis, particularly for clinically important but rare abnormalities. Existing rebalancing and logit adjustment methods mainly address class frequency while overlooking direction-dependent morphological variability across ECG classes. This study proposes Angular Gaussian Supervised Contrastive Learning (AG-SCL) for long-tailed multi-label ECG diagnosis. AG-SCL integrates three components into a unified framework: an Angular Gaussian contrastive branch that models full-covariance class uncertainty on unit-normalized embeddings, Adaptive Logit Adjustment that learns bounded label-state-specific prior corrections instead of fixed frequency-based margins, and tail-aware augmentation that generates morphology-preserving views while protecting the 7-25 Hz QRS-dominant band. The method was evaluated on the public PTB-XL benchmark and a nocturnal ECG dataset comprising 1317 hours of recordings from 141 subjects. AG-SCL achieved the best macro-level performance on both datasets. On PTB-XL, it obtained a balanced accuracy of 0.838, sensitivity of 0.709, specificity of 0.968, mean average precision of 0.495, and TPR at 5% FPR of 0.778. On Noc-ECG, the corresponding values were 0.918, 0.889, 0.947, 0.488, and 0.900. The largest gains occurred in rare or morphologically unstable rhythm classes, while ablation studies confirmed the contributions of full-covariance modelling, Adaptive Logit Adjustment, and tail-aware augmentation. AG-SCL improves long-tailed ECG diagnosis by combining prior calibration with anisotropic representation learning, enhancing sensitivity to rare arrhythmias while maintaining clinically relevant specificity. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Open-EXG/AG-SCL-for-Long-Tailed-ECG.

</details>


### [142] [Beyond Entropy: Correctness-Aware Advantage Shaping via Contrastive Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14614)
*Weiwen Xu,Jia Liu,Hou Pong Chan,Long Li,Deng Cai,Min Chen,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对比策略优化（CPO），通过参考引导与原始生成分布之间的对比差异来替代熵作为优势塑形信号，在RLVR中更准确地指示token级正确性，显著优于基于熵的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵在RLVR中无法区分有用不确定性与有害混淆，限制了其作为正确性信号的有效性。

Method: 提出对比策略优化（CPO），利用参考引导分布与原始生成分布之间的token级对比差异进行正确性感知的优势塑形。

Result: 理论和实验表明对比差异可靠指示token级正确性；CPO在域内和域外基准上显著优于基于熵的RLVR方法，同时保持强泛化能力；正确和错误响应分别自然支持探索与利用，平衡两者可获得最佳性能。

Conclusion: CPO解决了零优势问题，且On-policy Distillation是CPO的一个特例。对比差异是比熵更有效的正确性信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) commonly uses entropy for advantage shaping. However, entropy cannot distinguish useful uncertainty from detrimental confusion, limiting its effectiveness as a correctness signal. We propose Contrastive Policy Optimization (CPO), which uses token-level contrastive disagreement between reference-guided and vanilla generation distributions for correctness-aware advantage shaping. Both theoretical and empirical results show that this disagreement reliably indicates token-level correctness. We further show that On-policy Distillation is a special case of CPO, where the posterior distribution is instantiated by an external teacher model. CPO also resolves the zero-advantage problem. Experiments on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks demonstrate that CPO substantially outperforms entropy-based RLVR methods while maintaining strong generalization. Further analysis shows that correct and incorrect responses naturally support exploration and exploitation respectively, and balancing both leads to the best performance.

</details>


### [143] [PolyQ: Codesigning End-to-End Quantization Framework for Scalable Edge CPU LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14618)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,KyungIn Nam,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyQ提出了一种面向CPU的编译器与量化协同设计方案，通过按通道分配{2,3,4,8,16}比特位宽并在编译时进行通道重排与聚类，实现了在用户指定平均比特预算下的分数比特部署，在多个CPU平台上显著降低了激活重排开销并实现了接近线性的性能缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU是设备端LLM推理最通用的目标，但现有低比特量化方法要么只能提供粗粒度的操作点，要么提供细粒度混合精度但难以在CPU上高效执行。需要一种既能精细分配比特又能在CPU上高效运行的方案。

Method: PolyQ采用编译器/量化协同设计：1) 按通道分配{2,3,4,8,16}比特宽度的激活感知量化；2) 编译时将通道重排并聚类为比特同质块；3) 生成SIMD和LUT兼容的内核；4) 跨算子合并兼容的排列操作以减少运行时布局重排开销。

Result: 在Falcon-H1-3B、Llama2-13B和Qwen3-32B上的WikiText-2测试中，PolyQ在3-6比特范围内提供稳定的质量缩放，在3比特目标下困惑度比先前方法提升2.4-32.1%。在三种CPU（工作站、笔记本、移动端）上，编译器布局正则化减少激活重排流量达70.8%，预填延迟和解码吞吐量几乎与配置的比特预算成比例缩放，每个token的能量开销相比优化的LUT后端低于2%。

Conclusion: 分数比特CPU部署在实际中是可行的、可预测的且能效高的，适用于多样化的边缘目标设备。

Abstract: CPUs are the most universal target for on-device LLM inference, but existing low-bit quantization methods offer either coarse operating points or fine-grained mixed precision that is difficult to execute efficiently on CPUs. We present PolyQ, a CPU-oriented compiler/quantization co-design for activation-aware channel-wise bit allocation under a user-specified average-bit budget. PolyQ assigns per-channel bit-widths from $\{2,3,4,8,16\}$, then uses a compile-time model compiler to permute and cluster channels into bit-homogeneous blocks, generate SIMD- and LUT-compatible kernels, and merge compatible permutations across operators to keep layout regularization off the runtime path. This turns fine-grained budget fitting into a practical fractional-bit deployment method for CPU-only inference. Across Falcon-H1-3B, Llama2-13B, and Qwen3-32B on WikiText-2, PolyQ provides stable quality scaling from 3--6\,b and improves perplexity by 2.4--32.1\% over prior methods at a 3\,b target. End-to-end measurements on three representative CPUs -- workstation, laptop, and mobile -- show that compiler layout regularization reduces activation reorder traffic by up to 70.8\%, prefill latency and decode throughput scale nearly proportionally with the configured bit budget, and energy/token overhead stays below 2\% relative to an optimized LUT-based back-end. These results show that fractional-bit CPU deployment is practical, predictable, and energy-efficient across diverse edge targets.

</details>


### [144] [TIDE: Trustworthy and Interpretable Battery Degradation Estimation with Contextual Learning and Symbolic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14640)
*Wen Yang Tan,Jiawei Li,Fang Liu,Wei Zhang,Sumei Sun,Peng Cheng Wang,Elisa Y. M. Ang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TIDE框架，结合知识引导先验、单调残差建模和上下文学习，实现准确、可信、可解释的电池健康估计，相比基线平均提升19.7%的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池健康估计对电池管理系统至关重要，尤其在智能网联系统中，估计误差会在互联设备和下游决策中传播放大，而现有方法在准确性、可信度和可解释性方面难以兼顾。

Method: TIDE采用三组件骨干结构：(1)知识引导的退化先验提供可信估计；(2)单调残差组件提供与老化一致的精细修正；(3)上下文学习组件捕获电池特定操作效应。之后通过符号蒸馏将训练好的骨干压缩为简洁的符号代理模型，实现模型级可解释性。

Result: 相比代表性基线，TIDE整体估计精度平均提升19.7%；知识引导先验和单调残差建模显著减少了老化一致性违规，支持可信估计；骨干网络支持组件级解释，符号蒸馏提供紧凑的模型级表示。

Conclusion: TIDE在准确性、可信度和可解释性三个维度上均表现优异，适用于智能网联系统中的电池健康监测与决策支持。

Abstract: Battery health estimation is fundamental for battery management in battery-powered systems, where inaccurate health states may affect control, maintenance, and service life. It becomes even more critical in intelligent connected systems, where estimation errors can propagate across interconnected devices and downstream decisions. In this paper, we propose TIDE, a trustworthy and interpretable battery degradation estimator for reliable battery health estimation. TIDE jointly considers accuracy, trustworthiness, and interpretability, which are all essential for practical deployment and downstream decision making. To realize these objectives, TIDE combines battery-domain knowledge with operational measurements in a three-component backbone. A knowledge-guided degradation prior promotes trustworthy estimation, a monotone residual component provides interpretable aging-consistent refinement, and a contextual learning component captures battery-specific operational effects for improved accuracy. The trained backbone is then distilled into a compact symbolic surrogate that provides a concise model-level interpretation of its learned estimation logic. Experiments show that TIDE achieves strong estimation accuracy, improving overall estimation fidelity by an average of 19.7% over representative baselines. Its knowledge-guided prior and monotone residual modelling substantially reduce aging-consistency violations, supporting trustworthy estimation. Meanwhile, the backbone enables component-level interpretation, while symbolic distillation provides a compact model-level representation of the learned estimation logic. These results support the practical use of TIDE for battery health monitoring and decision support in intelligent connected systems.

</details>


### [145] [Trajectory-Aware Flow Matching for Topology Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14652)
*Shusheng Xiao,Jinshuai Bai,Hyogu Jeong,Yunfei Xi,Yilin Gui,YuanTong Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配的拓扑优化（FMTO）框架，通过轨迹感知的流匹配学习物理引导的优化历史，实现条件拓扑生成，在更少采样步数下生成多样、合规性能更优的拓扑候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化需重复有限元分析和灵敏度更新，在多变条件下生成多个候选设计时计算成本高昂；现有生成式TO模型依赖对抗训练、长反向扩散采样或外部引导，效率和稳定性不足。

Method: 提出FMTO框架：线性FMTO通过高斯源场与BESO参考拓扑插值作为基线；轨迹感知FMTO利用体积分数索引的BESO状态构建概率路径和目标速度场，将物理引导的优化历史融入生成流学习，无需推理时优化。同时提出路径-速度失配分析解释轨迹权重的影响。

Result: FMTO生成多样拓扑候选，在合规性能、体积分数满足度、拓扑保真度上优于扩散基线，且采样步数显著减少。在有限数据下，轨迹感知FMTO以中等轨迹权重取得最佳性能。轨迹锚点密度和三维拓扑生成实验验证了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的流匹配框架有效融合物理优化知识，提升了生成拓扑的质量和效率，可拓展至三维问题，为快速设计探索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Topology optimisation (TO) often requires repeated finite element analysis and sensitivity-based material updates, which can be costly when multiple candidate designs are needed under varying physical and design conditions. Generative TO offers a route to rapid design exploration, but existing models may rely on adversarial training, long reverse-diffusion sampling, or external guidance to maintain structural feasibility and physical consistency. This study develops a flow matching-based topology optimisation (FMTO) framework for conditional topology generation. Linear FMTO is first formulated as an endpoint-based baseline by interpolating between a Gaussian source field and the BESO reference topology. To introduce mechanically meaningful intermediate states, a trajectory-aware FMTO formulation is proposed, where volume-fraction-indexed BESO states are used to construct the probability path and target velocity field. This incorporates physics-guided optimisation history into generative flow learning without adding inference-time optimisation. A path--velocity mismatch analysis explains why moderate trajectory weighting can improve generation stability, whereas excessive guidance may over-constrain the learned transport. Numerical examples show that FMTO generates diverse topology candidates with improved compliance-related performance, volume-fraction satisfaction, topology fidelity, and substantially fewer sampling steps than a diffusion-based baseline. Under limited training data, trajectory-aware FMTO achieves the best overall performance with a moderate trajectory weight. Studies on trajectory-anchor density and three-dimensional topology generation further demonstrate the influence of path design and the applicability of the proposed framework beyond two-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [146] [Scalable Training of Continuous-Time Spiking Neural Networks with Differentiable Spike-Time Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14672)
*Yusuke Sakemi,Tomoya Takeuchi,Takeo Hosomi,Kazuyuki Aihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可微脉冲时间离散化（DSTD）的连续时间SNN训练框架，通过将不规则的脉冲输入映射到固定时间点的可微加权事件，将候选相关激活内存从O(N_out N_in)降低到O(N_out M)，并结合链式脉冲序列启发的正则化方法，在密集LIF层中实现了约100倍的内存减少和约20倍的训练加速，成功在单GPU上训练了9层CIFAR-10和20层Fashion-MNIST SNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练深度连续时间SNN受限于精确脉冲时间计算所需的大量内存，因为需要评估和保留由突触前脉冲排序决定的候选发放时间，这严重限制了可训练的网络深度和规模。

Method: 提出可微脉冲时间离散化（DSTD），将不规则脉冲映射到固定时间点的可微加权事件，用M个固定时间间隔替代输入相关的候选维度，精确近似连续时间膜电位动力学。同时引入链式脉冲序列启发的时序正则化，组织层间发放窗口，缓解死亡神经元问题，实现流水线式处理。

Result: 在密集LIF层中，DSTD将峰值内存消耗降低约100倍，训练时间降低约20倍（相比精确脉冲时间计算）。成功在单GPU上训练了9层卷积SNN（CIFAR-10）和20层卷积SNN（Fashion-MNIST）。

Conclusion: DSTD框架有效解决了连续时间SNN训练的内存瓶颈，大幅降低了计算开销，使得训练更深的连续时间SNN成为可能。

Abstract: Continuous-time spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide an event-driven framework for temporal computation, computational neuroscience, and neuromorphic hardware. However, training deep continuous-time SNNs is severely constrained by the memory required for exact spike-time computation, which evaluates and retains candidate firing times over intervals determined by presynaptic spike ordering. Here we introduce a memory-efficient training framework based on differentiable spike-time discretization (DSTD) for leaky integrate-and-fire neurons with general membrane and synaptic time constants. DSTD maps irregular presynaptic spikes onto differentiable weighted events at fixed time points, replacing the input-dependent candidate dimension with $M$ fixed time intervals while accurately approximating continuous-time membrane-potential dynamics. This reduces candidate-related activation memory from $O(N_{\mathrm{out}}N_{\mathrm{in}})$ to $O(N_{\mathrm{out}}M)$ in the case of time-to-first-spike (TTFS) coding, where $N_{\mathrm{in}}$ and $N_{\mathrm{out}}$ denote the numbers of presynaptic and postsynaptic neurons, respectively. We further introduce synfire-chain-inspired temporal regularization that organizes layer-wise firing windows, mitigates dead-neuron failures, and enables pipeline-like processing. In dense LIF layers, DSTD reduced peak memory consumption by up to approximately 100-fold and training time by up to approximately 20-fold compared with exact spike-time computation. Together, these methods allowed us to train 9-layer convolutional SNNs on CIFAR-10 and 20-layer convolutional SNNs on Fashion-MNIST on a single GPU.

</details>


### [147] [Grad2Fair: A Gradient-driven Approach for Graph Fairness without Demographics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14705)
*Yuchang Zhu,Zezhong Xie,Huizhe Zhang,Huazhen Zhong,Jintang Li,Liang Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GradDist（基于梯度的公平性度量指标）和Grad2Fair（梯度引导的无人口统计信息的图公平性方法），通过利用模型梯度分布中隐含的人口统计信息来量化并缓解图神经网络中的群体公平性问题，避免了传统方法需要预先获取或预测人口统计信息的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络常面临群体公平性问题，但现有大多数方法假设人口统计信息完全可用；少数尝试使用预测人口统计作为代理的方法因预测不准确而效果不佳。因此，需要在不依赖人口统计信息的情况下解决图公平性问题。

Method: 首先提出GradDist，通过测量误分类节点梯度分布中局部模式之间的距离来量化偏差；然后提出Grad2Fair，直接利用梯度信息进行去偏，消除了人口统计预测环节。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，Grad2Fair在大多数情况下优于现有基线方法，能够实现稳定的公平性性能提升。

Conclusion: 梯度分布中隐含着人口统计信息，直接利用梯度进行去偏可以避免依赖或预测人口统计信息，从而实现更稳定有效的群体公平性改善。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) frequently encounter group fairness issues, often yielding biased predictions against specific demographic groups defined by sensitive attributes such as gender or race. While this challenge has motivated extensive research, most existing solutions rely on the strong assumption that demographics are fully available. To bypass this strict requirement, a few recent studies have attempted to use predicted demographics as proxies to enforce fairness constraints. However, predicted demographics may be inaccurate, resulting in the failure to improve fairness. In this work, we investigate the problem of graph fairness without demographic information and avoid the utilization of predicted demographics. Motivated by our observation that the gradient distributions of misclassified nodes implicitly encode demographic information, we first propose GradDist, a gradient-based metric that quantifies bias by measuring the distance between local modes within these distributions. To mitigate this bias, we propose Gradient-to-Fairness (Grad2Fair), a gradient-guided approach for group fairness without demographics. Due to the potential demographics in gradients, Grad2Fair directly leverages gradients to debias and eliminates demographic prediction, thereby enabling stable fairness performance. Experiments on several real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of Grad2Fair, as evidenced by superior performance over baselines in most cases. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZzoomD/Grad2Fair.

</details>


### [148] [MESHA: Mechanism-Enforced Sequential Halving for Strategic Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14706)
*Xin Li,Zixin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MESHA算法，用于解决策略性线性老虎机中的最优臂识别问题。该算法采用均匀采样规则和逐轮格里姆触发条件（GTC），以应对臂可能策略性误报特征向量的行为。理论分析证明了MESHA在固定预算下的失败概率上界，并展示了基于G-最优设计的传统算法在策略性环境中的失效问题。实验表明MESHA优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略性线性老虎机环境中，每个臂可能为了最大化自己被识别为最优臂的概率而策略性地误报其特征向量。现有BAI算法（尤其是基于G-最优设计的算法）在设计时未考虑这种策略性行为，导致在策略性环境中失效。因此需要设计一种能够抵御臂的策略性误报行为、同时有效识别最优臂的算法。

Method: MESHA算法包含两个核心设计：(1) 朴素均匀采样规则，用于降低臂策略性行为的影响；(2) 逐轮格里姆触发条件（GTC），用于剔除报告特征严重偏离真实值的臂。算法在固定预算T下运行，并基于任意纳什均衡进行分析。

Result: 理论方面：证明任何臂为通过GTC检查并最大化被识别概率，会策略性地调整行为；推导出MESHA在固定预算T下失败概率的上界；证明基于G-最优设计的线性BAI算法在策略性环境中会因OD采样规则导致最优臂饥饿问题（被分配零采样预算）。实验方面：MESHA优于依赖OD采样规则的基线和特征无关的基线方法。

Conclusion: MESHA通过均匀采样加GTC触发条件的机制，有效解决了策略性线性老虎机中的最优臂识别问题，克服了传统基于最优设计方法在策略性环境中的根本性缺陷，为策略性在线学习问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We design and analyze \underline{M}echanism-\underline{E}nforced \underline{S}equential \underline{HA}lving (MESHA), an algorithm for Best Arm Identification (BAI) in strategic linear bandits. In this setting, each arm may strategically misreport its feature vector to maximize the probability of being identified as the best arm, when rewards are generated from the arms' true but unobservable features. The design of MESHA applies the naïve uniform sampling rule and an epoch-wise Grim Trigger Condition (GTC): the former reduces the impact of arms' strategic behaviours and the latter eliminates arms whose reported features severely deviate from the ground truth. Considering an arbitrary Nash Equilibrium, we prove that any arm would attempt to pass the GTC check to maximize its identified probability and derive an upper bound on the failure probability of MESHA within a fixed budget $T$. We also show that state-of-the-art linear BAI algorithms with $G$-optimal design would fail in such strategic environment, as the optimal design (OD)-based sampling rule based on strategically reported features may {\it starve} the optimal arm of any sampling budget. Finally, extensive numerical experiments indicate that MESHA
  outperforms baselines that rely on OD-based sampling rules as well as the feature-agnostic baselines, corroborating the efficacy of MESHA.

</details>


### [149] [Counterfactuals for Feature-Weighted Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14719)
*Richard J. Fawley,Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VoICE是一个针对加权k-means聚类的反事实解释框架，通过将反事实生成转化为向目标聚类的加权Voronoi区域投影，解决了聚类反事实解释的有效性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释在监督学习中已成熟，但扩展到无监督聚类存在困难：聚类标签无标注且由分区几何决定。现有基于成对质心边界的方法无法保证反事实样本落入目标簇内。

Method: VoICE框架：1) 将反事实生成转化为向目标簇加权Voronoi区域的投影；2) 将特征权重直接融入聚类几何和目标函数；3) 目标区域与数据边界相交并同伦收缩至质心，限制外推和边界敏感性；4) 支持可行动性约束下的最简解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，VoICE一致产生有效的目标簇成员关系，而主流的成对基线方法无法做到。

Conclusion: VoICE通过Voronoi-induced框架提供了聚类场景下有效、可行动且简洁的反事实解释，优于现有基于成对质心边界的方法。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations provide local, interpretable insight by identifying changes to an input that would alter its assigned outcome. Although well established in supervised learning, their extension to clustering is less direct, since cluster assignments are unlabeled and governed by the geometry of the partition. This paper introduces VoICE, a Voronoi-Induced Counterfactual Explainability framework for feature-weighted $k$-means clustering. Rather than treating cluster change as a crossing of a single pairwise centroid boundary, VoICE formulates counterfactual generation as projection onto the full weighted Voronoi region of a target cluster, incorporating feature weights directly into both the clustering geometry and the counterfactual objective to yield least-cost and parsimonious explanations under actionability constraints. Target regions are further intersected with data-derived bounds and homothetically contracted towards their centroids, limiting extrapolation and boundary sensitivity. VoICE consistently produces valid target-cluster membership, across several benchmark datasets, where the leading pairwise baseline does not.

</details>


### [150] [ChronoQG: Towards a Temporally Expressive and Hop-Bounded Benchmark for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14770)
*Xuemeng Liu,Zhengpin Li,Wanpeng Tang,Haotong Xie,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoQG框架，首个面向时间知识图谱的忠实问题生成基准，构建了16,011个带时间约束的问题，揭示现有方法在保持时间约束方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问题生成（KGQG）基准基于静态知识图谱，未编码图事实的时间范围，无法评估生成问题是否忠实保持了时间有效性、事件顺序和答案决定性的时间约束。

Method: 提出ChronoQG框架，包含时间约束分类体系、拓扑-时间子图采样和轨迹溯源问题生成三个组件，从异质时间知识图谱中构建四个基准数据集，共16,011个验证问题。

Result: 现有方法在保持时间约束方面表现不佳，尤其在多约束设置和困难时间约束类型下差距明显，揭示了静态KGQG与TKGQG之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: ChronoQG可作为时间忠实问题生成的挑战性测试平台，建立静态KGQG到TKGQG的桥梁，为未来研究提供基准。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) aims to generate natural-language questions from structured graph evidence. Existing KGQG benchmarks, however, are mostly built on static knowledge graphs and do not encode the temporal scopes of graph facts. As a result, they cannot evaluate whether generated questions faithfully preserve temporal validity, event ordering, and answer-determining temporal constraints. In this paper, we study temporal knowledge graph question generation (TKGQG), where a generated question must be faithful to both the support subgraph and the temporal constraints required to identify the target answer. We propose ChronoQG, the first temporally expressive and hop-bounded benchmark construction framework for TKGQG. ChronoQG integrates a comprehensive temporal-constraint taxonomy, topology-temporal subgraph sampling, and trace-grounded question generation to construct temporally faithful questions. The framework produces four benchmark datasets from heterogeneous temporal knowledge graphs, totaling 16,011 verified questions. We evaluate representative LLM-based KGQG methods and prompting baselines across diverse TKGQG settings, including temporal-constraint counts, topological templates, and temporal-constraint types. The results show that existing methods struggle to preserve temporal constraints, especially under multi-constraint settings and harder temporal-constraint types. These findings reveal a clear gap between static KGQG and TKGQG, and establish ChronoQG as a challenging testbed for temporally faithful question generation.

</details>


### [151] [Evaluating Epistemic Uncertainty: Beyond OOD Detection and Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14817)
*Jakub Paplhám,Willem Waegeman,Eyke Hüllermeier,Vojtěch Franc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于认知拒绝选择框架，提出了一种评估认知不确定性的决策理论方法，证明了最优选择器是真实偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值凸组合，并指出标准相关指标无法预测分解的实际效用，建议采用风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面作为诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的认知不确定性评估依赖OOD检测和主动学习等代理任务，但这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用评分不一致，缺乏直接评估认知不确定性对减少可减少误差（遗憾）能力的理论框架。

Method: 将选择性预测建模为覆盖度、期望风险和遗憾的约束优化问题，推导最优选择器形式；提出用可达到的风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面作为解耦和实用性的联合诊断工具；在密集人工标注数据集上进行基准测试。

Result: 决策理论排名与代理任务排名可能存在显著差异，包括方法间的成对排名反转（某些方法在一个标准上排名最高，在另一个标准上排名最低）。

Conclusion: 标准相关指标无法预测不确定性分解的实际可操作性，应采用决策理论导向的评估方法；现有不确定性解耦文献存在基础性缺陷。

Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

</details>


### [152] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称峰值感知损失（APAL），一种针对时序预测中低估惩罚更重且关注峰值区域的损失函数，并配套峰值关键评估协议，在行人需求预测等峰值关键场景下显著提升尾部精度和峰值预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人群需求预测等实际时序应用中，低估风险远高于高估，且罕见需求尖峰的准确预测对下游任务至关重要。然而现有时序预测模型多采用对称目标函数（MSE、MAE）且主要关注聚合误差，无法很好地捕捉极端值和峰值时序预测的失败情况。

Method: 提出APAL损失函数，包含两个设计：(i) 对低估给予更高惩罚；(ii) 增加预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重。同时提出峰值关键评估协议，在MAE/MSE基础上补充通道级尾部误差（Top-10%、Top-1%）和峰值指标（精确率、召回率、F1及时序容差下的峰值时序误差）。

Result: 在五个SOTA骨干网络上的长 horizon 多变量预测实验（使用墨尔本行人小时计数数据集和海滩游客数据集）及额外基准测试中，APAL在峰值关键数据集和设置下提升了尾部精度和峰值预测质量，同时暴露了与聚合误差之间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: APAL在峰值预测失败是主要操作关注点的场景下，是一种实用的解决方案，能够在控制聚合误差权衡的同时有效提升峰值预测能力。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [153] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究回合制随机博弈（TBSG）中可达性目标的去中心化与私有信息学习问题，在放松公开信息和中心化学习等强假设条件下，首次获得了正面的PAC学习结果，并提出了期望条件距离（ECD）参数的博弈论推广，建立了多项式样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可达性目标是强化学习中最基础的目标，但在马尔可夫决策过程和回合制随机博弈中，PAC学习可达性在无额外假设下是不可能的。以往TBSG的PAC学习研究依赖于两个强假设：(a) 玩家之间共享公开信息；(b) 中心化学习（玩家使用相同学习算法）。本文旨在放松这些不切实际的假设，研究在私有信息和去中心化学习条件下是否仍能实现可学习性。

Method: 本文提出了去中心化且私有信息的TBSG学习框架，其中两个玩家不共享信息也不使用相同算法。引入博弈论版本的期望条件距离（ECD）参数，该参数衡量到达目标集的期望步数。基于此参数，设计了具有多项式样本复杂度的学习算法，样本复杂度关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数、误差容忍度和失败概率的倒数呈多项式关系。

Result: 首次证明了在去中心化和私有信息条件下，TBSG中可达性目标是可PAC学习的；建立了多项式样本复杂度界，依赖于状态数、动作数、ECD参数及误差容忍度和失败概率的倒数。

Conclusion: 本文放松了以往工作中公开信息和中心化学习的强假设，为TBSG中可达性目标的去中心化私有信息学习提供了首个正面结果。引入的ECD参数的博弈论推广为后续研究提供了新的度量工具。

Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

</details>


### [154] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在小型精选数据集上微调语言模型会导致跨领域的意识形态泛化，即训练模型在一个领域的观点会系统性地迁移到无关领域，且微调相比少样本提示会放大这种偏移，甚至产生极端输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索微调语言模型时，使用狭窄、事实可辩护、通过审核的数据是否会导致模型在无关领域产生广泛的意识形态偏移，以及这种偏移的机制和影响范围。

Method: 在GPT-4.1和Gemma-3上分别使用左倾和右倾的经济学问答数据进行微调，测试模型在刑事司法、环境、文化品味等无关领域的意识形态偏移；同时测试食品安全的科学-伪科学轴微调对用户错误健康信念的谄媚效应。提出测量广度（跨领域泛化范围）和放大效应（微调相对少样本提示的偏移放大）的方法。

Result: 微调导致跨领域的系统性意识形态偏移；少样本提示可指示偏移方向，但微调将模型推向更极端的位置，包括种族-IQ关联和政治暴力等远超出分布范围的输出。该效应在Gemma-3上复现，在无评判器和外部基准下保持，与通用数据混合后仍然存在，且GSM8K准确率变化在±1pp以内。

Conclusion: 微调狭窄、看似"安全"的数据集也会引发不可控的广泛意识形态泛化，且微调比少样本提示带来更强的偏移放大效应，这对语言模型的安全对齐和部署构成重要挑战。

Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

</details>


### [155] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于真值表逻辑结构对二分类器进行最优线性组合的方法，通过多维度推广分类校准函数分析凸化经验风险，给出了存在唯一全局最小点的充分条件，针对三个分类器列出所有解的情况，并推导了指数损失和对数损失下最优权重的显式解析公式，同时引入φ-前沿概念评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类器集成方法多依赖迭代优化，缺乏对解的存在性、唯一性及全局最优性的严格理论分析，尤其当分类器数量增大时可能不存在最小点。本文旨在建立基于逻辑结构（真值表）的严格理论框架，从数学上刻画凸化经验风险的最小点性质，并给出无需迭代的解析求解公式。

Method: 利用真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化，将分类器对数据空间的划分转化为等价类；通过多维推广分类校准函数对凸化经验风险进行严格分析；建立存在唯一全局最小点的充分条件；针对三个分类器枚举所有可能配置（唯一解、下确界、非唯一最小点）；推导指数损失和逻辑损失下最优权重的解析表达式；引入φ-前沿概念用于稳定性与数据质量评估。

Result: 给出了任意分类器列表下凸化经验风险存在唯一全局最小点的充分条件；完整枚举了三分类器场景下所有可能的解配置（唯一解/下确界/非唯一最小点）；获得了指数损失和逻辑损失下最优权重的显式解析公式，无需迭代优化；提出了φ-前沿用于评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上系统解决了基于真值表逻辑结构的分类器线性组合的最优性分析问题，在解的存在唯一性、解析求解等方面取得实质性进展，φ-前沿为模型评估提供了新的几何工具。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>


### [156] [Leveraging Instruction Tuning and Merging for Reasoning Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14895)
*Yu-Du Feng,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本方法，通过在推理语言模型上进行指令微调后与原始推理模型合并，有效利用已有监督微调数据提升模型在可验证和难验证领域（如编程、文本摘要）的性能，成本低于3美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型在数学和编程等可验证领域表现出色，但在缺乏可靠验证器的领域训练困难；同时存在大量带有人类编写解决方案的监督微调数据未被充分利用，因此希望找到高效利用这些数据进一步提升RLM性能的方法。

Method: 首先在推理语言模型上进行经典的指令微调（不包含推理轨迹的有监督微调），然后将指令微调后的模型与原始推理模型进行合并，从而恢复在目标领域上的推理行为。

Result: 该方法在可验证领域（如编程）和难以验证的领域（如文本摘要）均提升了RLM的性能，同时保持了模型在其他领域的能力，且成本极低（不到3美元）。

Conclusion: 通过指令微调与模型合并的简单组合，可以低成本且高效地利用现有监督微调数据进一步提升推理语言模型在多种领域上的表现，无需依赖可靠验证器。

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

</details>


### [157] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机对数缩放（RLS）——一种即插即用的后处理防御方法，通过输出随机缩放后的混淆分数来抵御黑盒基于分数的对抗攻击，同时保持模型准确率；并针对现有非随机化防御AAA设计了自适应攻击，揭示了其脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本威胁机器学习模型的可靠部署，现有黑盒攻击方法发展迅速，亟需可扩展、低成本且强大的防御方法。

Method: 1) RLS防御：对模型logits进行随机缩放后输出混淆分数，作为即插即用的后处理模块；2) 自适应攻击：针对非随机化黑盒防御AAA设计的新型攻击。

Result: RLS显著降低最先进黑盒基于分数攻击的成功率，同时保持模型精度并最小化置信度分数失真；自适应攻击成功暴露AAA防御的脆弱性。

Conclusion: RLS是一种有效且轻量的随机化防御方案，但非随机化防御（如AAA）易被自适应攻击攻破，提示防御设计需考虑更全面的威胁模型。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [158] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过逐步简化SOTA模型DynaMix，得到一个仅含两个参数的最小可解释模型DynaBase。该模型通过线性混合当前潜状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后继进行预测，尽管极度简单，却在混沌和循环系统的零样本动力学系统重构上表现优异。作者进一步推导出单参数映射族，连接了上下文鹦鹉算法与混沌行为，并揭示了不同训练策略对短期预测与DS重构的不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解零样本动力学系统重构的基础模型中的核心机制，剥离冗余的架构，揭示动力学系统领域中上下文学习的最小必要条件。

Method: 从SOTA模型DynaMix迭代简化到两参数模型DynaBase，该模型通过当前潜状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后继的线性混合进行预测。进一步推导出单参数映射族，并分析不同训练策略的影响。

Result: DynaBase在混沌和循环系统的零样本DS重构上取得高度竞争性的结果，参数数量比其他基础模型低数个数量级。允许直接优化DS重构指标，甚至可得到预测MSE的闭式解析解。

Conclusion: DynaBase揭示了产生零样本DS重构的最小机制，并在一个可理解的数学框架内调和了文献中看似矛盾的观察结果。

Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

</details>


### [159] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对序列观测数据中结果变量存在相互干扰（干扰效应）的因果推断问题，提出了一种基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的计算高效方法，用于学习包含伊辛模型依赖和低秩潜在混杂因子的马尔可夫模型，并证明了参数估计的非渐近一致性以及因果量的可靠估计，通过合成实验和COVID-19疫苗率对美国县级死亡率影响的案例验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观测性时序数据中，个体间的结果变量往往存在相互依赖（干扰效应），传统因果推断方法假设无干扰（SUTVA）不成立。本文旨在解决这种情境下的因果估计问题，同时处理时序依赖、干扰效应和潜在混杂因素。

Method: 将N个单位T个时间步的二元结果建模为马尔可夫过程，每步结果依赖通过伊辛模型刻画，外部场包含治疗效应和低秩因子结构的潜在混杂。基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）学习模型参数，在温和假设下建立非渐近一致性，并通过模型采样估计因果量。

Result: 理论证明了MPLE参数估计的非渐近一致性，并能忠实估计因果量。合成实验和COVID-19疫苗率对美国县级死亡率的真实案例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在时序观测数据中考虑结果干扰效应的因果推断框架，理论保证与实证结果均表明该方法有效，为处理复杂依赖结构的因果问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

</details>


### [160] [LongStraw: Long-Context RL Beyond 2M Tokens under a Fixed GPU Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14952)
*Changhai Zhou,Kieran Liu,Yuhua Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Harry Zhang,Irvine Lu,Nolan Ho,Lucian Li,Andrew Lei,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Yihang Zeng,Di Zhang,Rio Yang,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongStraw是一个面向百万级token RL后训练的架构感知执行栈，通过共享提示无梯度计算、仅保留必要模型状态、逐条重放短响应分支等策略，在固定GPU预算下实现了超长上下文的高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理上下文长度已接近百万token，但RL后训练的长度通常仅256K或更少，导致部署时依赖长度泛化。这一差距对AI Agent尤为关键，其观测、工具输出、文档和决策会随时间累积成长轨迹。因此需要一种能在固定GPU预算下支持百万级token RL后训练的方法。

Method: LongStraw采用架构感知的执行栈：(1) 共享提示部分不保留自动梯度；(2) 仅保留后续token所需的模型特定状态；(3) 短响应分支逐条重放，以增加重放时间为代价缩小活跃训练图。针对Qwen3.6-27B（混合循环+全注意力）和GLM-5.2（压缩注意力混合专家）实现。

Result: 在8块H20 GPU上，LongStraw完成了2.1M位置的分组Qwen评分与响应反向传播（组大小2和8）；增加组大小仅增加0.21 GB峰值显存；压力测试达到4.46M位置。在32块H20 GPU上，验证了GLM-5.2全部78层上2.1M token提示的端到端执行路径。

Conclusion: LongStraw建立了百万级token RL后训练的可行执行容量，但实验侧重于执行能力而非完整训练正确性，因为捕获的提示状态已被分离，部分分布式前向和梯度组合路径仍不完整。

Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

</details>


### [161] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多轴max@K，一种基于组的强化学习目标，用于提升文生图扩散模型在预定义语义模式上的覆盖度。该方法通过对每组样本取每个类别最大得分再求和的方式进行信用分配，使不同样本贡献于不同类别，从而改善图像多样性并减少人口统计偏倚。实验在合成数据和SD3.5-M上验证了信用分配机制，并在感知外观公平性上使公平分数提升0.23-0.36，同时保持图像质量和文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型在相同提示下生成的图像仅覆盖少数视觉不同的模式，导致多样性不足；对于以人物为中心的提示，这种局限还会反映或放大人口统计偏见。作者将该问题形式化为对预定义语义模式的覆盖（目标模式覆盖），并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出多轴max@K（multi-axis max@K），一种基于组的强化学习目标。给定一组样本和每个目标类别的一个分数，先对每个类别取组内样本的最大得分，再求和这些类别级最大值。信用分配机制使样本仅在增加某类别组内最大值时才获得该类别的正权重，从而允许多个样本分别贡献于不同类别。

Result: 在合成数据和SD3.5-M上使用确定性像素级颜色奖励验证了信用分配机制的有效性。在感知外观公平性任务上，使用三个自动评估器评估保留提示，多轴max@K使公平分数相对基础模型提升0.23-0.36，同时保持图像质量和文本对齐。

Conclusion: 多轴max@K能有效提升扩散T2I模型在预定义语义模式上的覆盖度，改善多样性并缓解人口统计偏见，且不影响图像质量和文本对齐能力。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [162] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MseaCL，一种多模态语义感知对比学习框架，通过利用放射学报告间的语义相似性来缓解医学影像-文本对比学习中的假阴性问题，在儿童脑肿瘤分子分类下游任务中AUC提升至少22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习在医学场景中假设数据批次内非配对样本均为负样本，但医学样本常共享高层语义属性（如相同疾病类型），导致假阴性样本污染，降低表征质量。

Method: 提出MseaCL框架，在儿科3D脑MRI与放射学报告的多模态对比学习中，引入放射学报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，动态调整采样权重，减轻语义相似假阴性样本的影响。

Result: 在儿科脑肿瘤分子分类下游任务中，AUC提升至少22.6%，表明框架能够学习更鲁棒且语义对齐的多模态表征。

Conclusion: MseaCL通过语义感知的对比学习有效缓解了医学场景下的假阴性问题，证明了语义引导在多模态医学表征学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [163] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kernel-WIS估计器，结合加权重要性采样的有界性和普通重要性采样的线性性，用于上下文赌博机的离线策略评估，在行为策略误设等复杂条件下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线策略评估方法（如普通重要性采样和加权重要性采样）各有优缺点：普通重要性采样无偏但方差大，加权重要性采样有界但存在偏差。本文旨在结合两者的优势，提出一个既保持有界性又具有线性性的新估计器。

Method: 提出Kernel-WIS估计器，该方法将加权重要性采样的有界属性与普通重要性采样的线性属性进行结合，利用核函数对重要性权重进行平滑处理。

Result: 理论证明了Kernel-WIS的渐近一致性；实验表明该方法在多种复杂条件下（包括行为策略误设）显著优于强基线方法（如普通加权重要性采样）。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS通过融合两种重要性采样的优势，实现了更稳定且有效的离线策略评估，特别适用于行为策略未知或存在误设的实际场景。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [164] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍SINDy方法及其扩展，从标准算法逐步推进到弱形式、集成、约束和参数化变体，并通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个工程案例展示其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多工程问题中控制方程未知或仅部分已知，神经网络等代理建模需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理解释性，SINDy方法通过稀疏回归从较小的数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，但该方法在工程问题中的应用文献较少。

Method: 采用标准SINDy算法进行稀疏回归，并逐步扩展至噪声鲁棒的弱形式、集成方法、约束形式和参数化形式；通过三个部分组织内容：标准算法及扩展介绍、无人机系统辨识案例、混沌热虹吸换热器案例。

Result: 通过无人机和混沌热虹吸换热器两个详细的工程案例研究，展示了SINDy方法易于实现且足够灵活，可作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具。

Conclusion: SINDy方法简单易实现且灵活，能够作为工程应用中的有效系统辨识工具，适合无先验知识的读者跟随学习并应用到自己的问题中。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [165] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在离线强化学习应用于治疗推荐时，利用协变量平衡诊断来检测隐藏混杂/模型误设。结果表明现有研究存在高偏倚风险或现有指标不足，无法保证统计稳健性，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在治疗推荐中可能受到隐藏混杂和模型误设的影响，导致结果偏差，因此需要有效的诊断方法来评估这些风险。

Method: 应用协变量平衡诊断来检测离线强化学习研究中是否存在隐藏混杂或模型误设问题。

Result: 现有离线强化学习治疗推荐研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究，导致无法确认统计稳健性。

Conclusion: 现有离线强化学习在治疗推荐中的应用缺乏统计稳健性，未来需要更方法论上鲁棒的研究方向。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [166] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LINCS范畴框架，通过修复非组合性（图表无法通过商草图分解）来形式化机器学习问题。将ML问题建模为草图（含交换条件、极限锥、余极限余锥），非组合性定义为通用分解问题的失败而非数值误差。核心思想是使用切空间提升（Tangent Lift）构造因子化障碍，并通过INC余代数的最终余代数寻找稳定不动点，从而将ML转化为余代数的不动点搜索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习形式化框架过度依赖标量化的损失函数或向量空间假设，缺乏处理非组合性（即结构分解失败）的数学框架。本文旨在用范畴论中的草图（Sketch）理论，为机器学习提供一种不依赖于数值误差、纯粹以结构分解失败定义非组合性的形式化方法。

Method: (1) 将ML问题定义为草图S=(S,D,L,K)，其中S为底层图，D为交换条件，L为极限锥，K为余极限余锥；(2) 定义基础缺陷Obs(Fact_S(D))为模型D因式分解的障碍；(3) 通过切空间函子T提升到切范畴，定义LINCS为Obs(Fact_S(TD))；(4) 引入INC余代数和切学习草图（带Cockett-Cruttwell切结构）；(5) 构造INC自函子，迭代切空间提升生成D,TD,T^2D,...的因子化问题塔；(6) 利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终INC余代数的存在性（当T_INC具有集合基类实现且能创建最终载体时）。

Result: 成功构建了LINCS范畴框架，提供了非组合性的纯结构定义；建立了切空间提升与因子化障碍之间的关系；证明了在特定条件下最终INC余代数存在；在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习等场景的实验正在进展中（由配套论文描述）。

Conclusion: LINCS框架将机器学习重新形式化为在切范畴中搜索余代数不动点的问题，为理解模型结构的组合性提供了范畴论基础。非组合性被严格定义为通用因子化问题的失败而非数值误差，这为ML理论提供了新的数学视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [167] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OPD²的新的同策略蒸馏方法，通过引入“delta信号”（教师模型与基础模型之间的差异）来替代直接模仿教师输出分布，显著提升了推理能力迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略蒸馏方法直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但忽略了推理调优带来的变化信号，因此希望设计一种更直接的信号来高效迁移推理能力。

Method: 定义delta信号为教师模型与其指令调优前的基础模型之间的差异，利用该差异作为蒸馏奖励，替代传统的输出分布直接模仿，形成On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)方法。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准上，OPD²持续优于传统同策略蒸馏方法，且只需较短的后期训练即可使推理LLM达到强性能。

Conclusion: Delta信号能够更有效地捕获推理调优带来的变化，为同策略蒸馏提供更优的监督信号，提升了蒸馏效率与效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [168] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，将已知ODE分量保留、未知分量用神经网络替代，通过RTS平滑器和反向传播交替迭代进行状态与参数估计，在部分状态观测下学习缺失的动力学组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际系统中部分动力学方程未知，且仅能测量部分状态变量，现有方法难以在保持可解释性的同时从未完整观测中学习缺失的ODE组件。

Method: 两阶段交替迭代：第一阶段固定模型参数，使用RTS平滑器从测量数据推断隐状态；第二阶段固定平滑轨迹，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数，迭代直至满足预设准则。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学基准系统上，该方法在部分状态观测下成功学习缺失ODE组件，改善了隐状态重建和长时域预测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能够利用并保留可解释的机制结构，在测量不完整的情况下有效学习缺失的动力学组件，提升状态估计和预测能力。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [169] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，系统研究了世界-动作模型（WAM）在面对对抗性攻击时的脆弱性。作者发现，通过施加微小的视觉扰动，可以破坏WAM中动作生成与未来世界预测之间的对齐关系，从而导致任务失败。BadWAM定义了两种攻击类型：动作攻击（直接驱动模型执行错误动作）和想象保持攻击（在保持未来预测看似合理的同时暗中改变动作）。实验表明这些攻击能显著降低模型任务成功率，例如动作攻击将成功率从96.5%降至43.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）通常被认为通过耦合动作生成与未来世界预测来提升鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性。然而，本文质疑了这一假设，旨在揭示WAM在面对精心设计的对抗性扰动时的脆弱性，特别是动作预测与世界预测之间的对齐关系是否容易被破坏。

Method: 本文提出BadWAM框架，从两个维度（攻击强度和隐蔽性）刻画攻击面。具体设计了两种攻击：1）动作攻击（action-only attack），直接驱动模型执行导致任务失败的动作；2）想象保持攻击（imagination-preserving attack），在保持模型预测的未来世界接近干净样本的同时，诱导有害的动作偏移。这两种攻击覆盖了从显式动作劫持到隐蔽性动作失同步的完整WAM特定失效谱系。

Result: 在多种WAM变体上的闭环执行实验表明，动作攻击将任务成功率从96.5%大幅降至43.1%；想象保持攻击进一步揭示了WAM特有的脆弱性：适度的未来保持正则化能够在维持强大攻击效果的同时，降低未来想象的漂移。

Conclusion: WAM中动作生成与未来世界预测之间的对齐关系并不如预期般鲁棒，可以通过对抗性攻击被有效破坏。这警示我们，依赖"想象未来检查动作"的安全机制存在根本性漏洞，需要重新思考WAM的安全保障方法。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [170] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于KP-tree的可变草图（Mutable Sketch）方法，通过在线实时更新用户嵌入向量，解决了二阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧问题。在KuaiRec数据集上，仅用1.8%的数据读取就达到了比ALS更好的RMSE（0.810 vs 0.822），且新用户首次评分后即可在<1ms内获得个性化推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决二阶段推荐系统中用户嵌入更新滞后（嵌入陈旧性）的问题，即在用户产生新评分后，其嵌入向量需等到下一个重训练周期才能更新，导致推荐质量下降。

Method: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketch）方法：将用户偏好存储在KP-tree（一种带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）中；仅需拟合一次低秩投影；当新评分到达时在线重新计算嵌入向量。同时证明了每次新观察都能单调收紧预测误差包络（定理1），这是FunkSVD和eALS所缺乏的保证。

Result: 在KuaiRec数据集上，可变草图以1.8%的数据读取量实现了0.810的RMSE，而ALS在100%数据读取量下为0.822；每批更新速度提升8倍；新用户首次评分后可在<1ms内获得个性化推荐，无需模型重训练。在稀疏数据（<1%密度）上，KP-tree的范数比例采样相比均匀采样能提升40-130%的物品覆盖率。

Conclusion: 可变草图方法能有效解决嵌入陈旧性问题，实现实时用户嵌入更新，在数据效率、更新速度和冷启动推荐方面均优于传统方法，且提供了理论上的误差保证。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [171] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体推文的新方法，用于分析比特币市场情绪。与价格预测模型不同，该工作专注于解释市场情绪，使用XGBoost分类器实现了约0.84的平均F1分数，并通过SHAP方法增强了模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具的广泛应用，理解其市场行为的需求日益增长。现有研究多聚焦于价格预测，而本文旨在解释市场情绪，填补了结合多源数据（链上数据、金融数据、社交媒体）进行情绪分析的研究空白。

Method: 将区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情绪分类相结合，把情绪趋势与链上和金融指标归一化为统一数据集。采用多种机器学习模型进行交叉验证，最终选择XGBoost作为分类模型，并使用SHAP方法量化各特征对模型预测的贡献。

Result: XGBoost模型在情绪分类任务中表现最佳，平均F1分数约为0.84。SHAP分析揭示了链上特征对模型预测的具体贡献，增强了模型透明度和可解释性。

Conclusion: 多源数据组合能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞察，为数据驱动的加密货币分析提供了有效支持，并指出了未来使用深度学习进行改进的方向。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Generalized Neural Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14122)
*Natan Hilario da Silva,Vicente Garibay Cancho,Adriano Kamimura Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义神经分布回归（GNDR）框架，将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布的参数空间，通过两步半参数估计过程解决深度架构的非可辨识性与最大似然理论的兼容性问题，利用解析Fisher信息矩阵和多元Delta方法实现不确定量化，在多种数据模态上展示了优越的分布校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习模型虽然在点预测上表现优异，但缺乏严格的不确定性量化能力；而经典统计分布模型虽能提供概率解释，但难以融入现代深度架构。如何将深度神经网络的表达能力与经典统计分布的严谨不确定性量化有机统一，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出GNDR框架，将DNN嵌入概率分布的参数空间；设计两步半参数估计：先训练上游网络作为固定非线性基展开，再独立优化终端预测头；基于分离后的结构提取解析Fisher信息矩阵，结合多元Delta方法生成观测特定的置信带和容忍区间。

Result: 在过离散临床计数数据、右删失转录组生存数据（混合治愈框架）以及从无结构人脸图像直接估计的零截断年龄分布等多种数据模态上，GNDR框架展示了优越的分布校准性能。

Conclusion: GNDR框架成功弥合了深度学习与经典统计推断之间的鸿沟，为深度预测模型提供了严格的、可操作的统计推断能力，且已开源实现于Python包thetaflow中。

Abstract: We introduce the Generalized Neural Distributional Regression (GNDR) framework, which seamlessly embeds deep neural networks into the parameter space of classical probability distributions. To reconcile the inherent non-identifiability of deep architectures with maximum likelihood theory, we propose a two-step semi-parametric estimation procedure. By isolating the terminal prediction heads and treating the upstream network as a fixed, non-linear basis expansion, GNDR enables the extraction of analytical Fisher Information matrices. This facilitates rigorous uncertainty quantification, generating observation-specific confidence bands and tolerance intervals via the multivariate Delta method. We demonstrate the framework's versatility and superior distributional calibration across diverse data modalities, including overdispersed clinical counts, right-censored transcriptomic survival profiles under a mixture cure framework, and zero-truncated age distributions derived directly from unstructured facial images. The methodology is natively implemented in the open-source Python package \textit{thetaflow}.

</details>


### [173] [Operator-Informed Gaussian Processes for Complex Helmholtz Wavefields: From Synthetic Benchmarks to In Vivo Brain Elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14193)
*Boyuan Deng,Kshitiz Upadhyay,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将算子条件高斯过程回归扩展至复值Helmholtz问题，通过实化复算子实现标准实值GP推断，在低计算预算下优于有限差分和神经网络基线，并返回波场后验概率分布而非点估计。应用于脑磁共振弹性成像时，多尺度先验达到了0.77的相关性，但后验不确定性尚未校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helmholtz方程控制时谐波传播，耗散介质中复模量使波数平方为复数。从稀疏含噪数据推断波场需要能量化不确定性的求解器，而现有算子条件GP回归主要针对实值场，缺乏对复值问题的扩展。

Method: 将复Helmholtz算子实化为等价的耦合实块系统，从而利用标准实值GP条件化进行推断。设计从对角先验到多尺度变体的先验族，基于PDE残差和边界迹进行条件化。

Result: 在一至三维基准问题上，以远少于有限差分和神经网络基线的内部约束点取得竞争性精度。应用于活体脑磁共振弹性成像时，多尺度先验重建的剪切旋度场与测量相关性达0.77（目标0.75），增益源于多尺度核而非实-虚耦合。

Conclusion: 模型失配导致低频精度上限，后验不确定性尚未校准。校准不确定性是耗散介质中概率波场推断的核心下一步。

Abstract: The Helmholtz equation governs time-harmonic wave propagation, and in dissipative media a complex modulus renders its squared wavenumber $κ^2$ complex. Inferring such fields from sparse, noisy data calls for solvers that also quantify their own uncertainty. Physics-informed Gaussian-process (GP) regression supplies this by returning a posterior over the solution, yet operator-conditioned formulations have been developed almost exclusively for real-valued fields. We extend operator-informed GP regression to complex-valued Helmholtz problems by realifying the complex operator into an equivalent coupled real block, which enables inference with standard real-valued GP conditioning. The construction admits a family of priors, from a proper diagonal prior to coregionalized and multiscale variants, and conditions on PDE residuals and boundary traces. On benchmark problems in one to three dimensions, the solver is competitive with finite-difference and neural-network baselines at a far smaller interior-constraint budget. Unlike those deterministic baselines, it returns a posterior over the complex wavefield rather than a point estimate. Applied to \textit{in vivo} brain magnetic resonance elastography, a proper multiscale prior reconstructs the shear curl field to a correlation of $0.77$ with measurement, above a $0.75$ target. The gain arises from the multiscale kernel rather than from real--imaginary coupling. We further identify a low-frequency accuracy ceiling set by model mismatch and a posterior uncertainty that is not yet calibrated. Calibrated uncertainty therefore emerges as the central next step for probabilistic wavefield inference in dissipative media.

</details>


### [174] [Spectral Concentration and Recovery in Sparse High-Dimensional Random Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14304)
*Manuel Fernandez,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维随机几何图的谱性质与潜在几何恢复。针对球面模型和高斯模型，在更弱假设下改进了谱范数界，实现了潜在向量与Gram矩阵的精确恢复，并首次证明了高斯混合块模型在最优连通性尺度下的精确恢复结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在高维随机几何图的谱范数界和潜在几何恢复方面存在假设过强或恢复条件不充分的问题。本文旨在弱化假设、改进谱界，并首次建立高斯混合块模型的精确恢复理论。

Method: 结合正交多项式展开、解耦技术和矩阵浓度不等式，避免使用迹矩论证。对于球面模型在np=Ω(logn)尺度下推导谱范数界；通过前导特征空间恢复潜在向量和相对Gram矩阵；利用半定规划在最优连通性下实现高斯混合块模型的精确标签恢复。

Result: 球面模型谱范数界改进为O(√(nplogn)+npτ)；潜在向量和Gram矩阵恢复条件为d≪nplog(1/p)/logn，球面模型仅需d≫log(1/p)，高斯模型需d≫log²(1/p)logn；在高斯混合块模型中，于最优连通性np=Ω(logn)下，半定规划在中等分离区域实现精确恢复。

Conclusion: 本文在更弱假设下得到了更优的谱范数界和几何恢复保证，首次证明了高斯混合块模型的精确恢复结果，且所用方法避开了迹矩论证，为相关领域提供了更通用的理论工具。

Abstract: We study sparse random geometric graphs generated by connecting pairs of high-dimensional vectors whose inner product exceeds a threshold. The latent vectors are sampled either uniformly from the sphere or from a standard Gaussian distribution. Although every edge appears with probability $p$, the edges are dependent through their shared latent vectors. For the spherical model, at the connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, we prove $\|A-\mathbb E A\|=O\left(\sqrt{np\log n}+npτ\right)$, with high probability, where $τ$ is the cap threshold. This sharpens the spectral norm bound of Liu, Mohanty, Schramm, and Yang (2023) under weaker assumptions. An analogous result holds for the Gaussian model after removing the fluctuations of the vector norms, yielding improved global synchronization guarantees for the homogeneous Kuramoto model. We then recover the latent geometry from the leading eigenspace. When $np\gg\log n$, both the latent vector and relative Gram matrix errors vanish provided $d\ll np\log(1/p)/\log n$. The required lower dimension is only $d\gg\log(1/p)$ for the spherical model and $d\gg\log^2(1/p)\log n$ for the Gaussian model, improving the recovery guarantees of Li and Schramm (2023). Finally, we prove the first exact recovery result for the Gaussian mixture block model of Li and Schramm (2023). At the optimal connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, a polynomial-time semidefinite program exactly recovers all labels in a moderate-separation regime, whereas larger separation makes exact recovery impossible because isolated vertices appear with high probability. Our proofs combine orthogonal polynomial expansions, decoupling, and matrix concentration, avoiding the trace-moment arguments used in previous work.

</details>


### [175] [Optimal Self-Distillation for Rectified Flow via Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14947)
*Saptarshi Roy,Debepsita Mukherjee,Pratik Patil*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了整流流（RF）中的最优自蒸馏（SD）方法：在给定次优教师速度场的情况下，通过混合真实RF速度和教师速度训练学生模型，证明了在线性RF和岭正则化条件下，最优混合系数存在闭式解，且当教师风险沿正则化路径非平稳时，学生模型严格优于教师。最优系数遵循符号规则：正混合校正欠正则化教师，负混合校正过正则化教师。实验验证了该方法在速度风险、模态恢复和有限步生成方面的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型越来越多地使用模型生成信号进行训练，这既带来了自我改进的机会，也存在崩溃的风险。本文旨在研究在整流流框架下，如何通过最优自蒸馏策略，利用次优教师模型来训练学生模型，从而实现可证明的性能改进。

Method: 针对带岭正则化的线性整流流，在固定插值对条件下，推导了精确的仿射路径恒等式，得到了最优混合系数的闭式解。提出了单次广义交叉验证（GCV）和验证调优程序，避免了对混合权重的网格搜索和重复重拟合。结合RF Wasserstein收敛界，证明了最优自蒸馏提升了对连续时间和有限步生成误差起控制作用的速度估计项。
